version: 2
updates:
  - package-ecosystem: "nuget"
    directory: "/"
    schedule:
      interval: "weekly"
    open-pull-requests-limit: 10
    groups:
      avalonia:
        patterns: ["Avalonia*"]
      microsoft:
        patterns: ["Microsoft.*"]
      opentelemetry:
        patterns: ["OpenTelemetry*"]
      testing:
        patterns: ["xunit*", "Shouldly*", "NSubstitute*", "coverlet*"]
    ignore:
      - dependency-name: "FluentAssertions"
      - dependency-name: "Moq"

  - package-ecosystem: "dotnet-sdk"
    directory: "/"
    schedule:
      interval: "weekly"

  - package-ecosystem: "github-actions"
    directory: "/"
    schedule:
      interval: "weekly"
    open-pull-requests-limit: 5


================================================================================
FILE: .github/workflows/build-and-release.yml
SIZE: 11.04 KB
================================================================================

name: Build and Release

on:
  push:
    branches: [master, main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [master, main]

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: ${{ github.event_name == 'pull_request' }}

env:
  DOTNET_NOLOGO: true
  DOTNET_CLI_TELEMETRY_OPTOUT: true
  DOTNET_SKIP_FIRST_TIME_EXPERIENCE: true

jobs:
  build-and-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Setup Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Setup .NET
        uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          global-json-file: global.json

      - name: Install Android workload
        shell: bash
        run: |
          for attempt in 1 2 3; do
            dotnet workload install android && exit 0
            sleep $((attempt * 20))
          done
          exit 1

      - name: Setup Keystore
        id: keystore
        shell: bash
        run: |
          if [[ -n "${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}" ]]; then
            echo "${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}" | base64 -d > "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore"
            echo "path=$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
            echo "available=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          else
            keytool -genkey -v -keystore "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore" \
              -alias myalias -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 1 \
              -storepass dummypassword -keypass dummypassword \
              -dname "CN=Dummy, OU=Dummy, O=Dummy, L=Dummy, ST=Dummy, C=US" \
              2>/dev/null || true
            echo "path=$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
            echo "available=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          fi

      - name: Restore
        shell: bash
        run: |
          for attempt in 1 2 3; do
            dotnet restore -property:Configuration=Release && exit 0
            sleep $((attempt * 20))
          done
          exit 1

      - name: Build
        run: |
          SIGN_PASS="${{ secrets.ANDROID_SIGNING_PASSWORD }}"
          if [[ "${{ steps.keystore.outputs.available }}" != "true" ]]; then
            SIGN_PASS="dummypassword"
          fi
          dotnet build --configuration Release --no-restore \
            -p:BuildNumber=${{ github.run_number }} \
            -p:AndroidKeyStore=true \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyStore="${{ steps.keystore.outputs.path }}" \
            -p:AndroidSigningStorePass="$SIGN_PASS" \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyPass="$SIGN_PASS" \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyAlias=myalias

      - name: Test
        run: dotnet test --test-modules "tests/*/bin/Release/net11.0/*.Tests.dll"

  build-desktop:
    needs: build-and-test
    if: github.event_name == 'push'
    timeout-minutes: 30
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - os: ubuntu-latest
            rid: linux-x64
          - os: ubuntu-latest
            rid: linux-arm64
          - os: windows-latest
            rid: win-x64
          - os: windows-latest
            rid: win-arm64
          - os: macos-latest
            rid: osx-x64
          - os: macos-latest
            rid: osx-arm64
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Setup .NET
        uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          global-json-file: global.json

      - name: Publish
        shell: bash
        run: |
          for attempt in 1 2 3; do
            dotnet publish src/MyAdventure.Desktop/MyAdventure.Desktop.csproj \
              --configuration Release \
              -r ${{ matrix.rid }} \
              --self-contained true \
              -p:PublishSingleFile=true \
              -p:IncludeNativeLibrariesForSelfExtract=true \
              -p:Version=1.0.${{ github.run_number }} \
              --output ./publish && exit 0
            sleep $((attempt * 20))
          done
          exit 1

      - name: Create archive (Unix)
        if: runner.os != 'Windows'
        shell: bash
        run: |
          cd ./publish
          tar -czvf ../MyAdventure-${{ matrix.rid }}.tar.gz .

      - name: Create archive (Windows)
        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          Compress-Archive -Path ./publish/* -DestinationPath ./MyAdventure-${{ matrix.rid }}.zip

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: ${{ matrix.rid }}
          path: |
            ./MyAdventure-${{ matrix.rid }}.tar.gz
            ./MyAdventure-${{ matrix.rid }}.zip
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 7

  build-android:
    needs: build-and-test
    if: github.event_name == 'push'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v7

      - name: Setup Java
        uses: actions/setup-java@v6
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Setup .NET
        uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v6
        with:
          global-json-file: global.json

      - name: Install Android workload
        shell: bash
        run: |
          for attempt in 1 2 3; do
            dotnet workload install android && exit 0
            sleep $((attempt * 20))
          done
          exit 1

      - name: Setup Keystore
        id: keystore
        shell: bash
        run: |
          if [[ -n "${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}" ]]; then
            echo "${{ secrets.ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}" | base64 -d > "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore"
            echo "path=$GITHUB_WORKSPACE/android.keystore" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
            echo "available=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          else
            echo "available=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          fi

      - name: Build Android APK (Signed)
        if: steps.keystore.outputs.available == 'true'
        shell: bash
        run: |
          dotnet publish src/MyAdventure.Android/MyAdventure.Android.csproj \
            --configuration Release \
            -p:ApplicationVersion=${{ github.run_number }} \
            -p:ApplicationDisplayVersion=1.0.${{ github.run_number }} \
            -p:AndroidKeyStore=true \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyStore="${{ steps.keystore.outputs.path }}" \
            -p:AndroidSigningStorePass="${{ secrets.ANDROID_SIGNING_PASSWORD }}" \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyPass="${{ secrets.ANDROID_SIGNING_PASSWORD }}" \
            -p:AndroidSigningKeyAlias=myalias \
            -p:AndroidUseAapt2Daemon=false \
            --output ./publish/android

      - name: Build Android APK (Debug key)
        if: steps.keystore.outputs.available != 'true'
        shell: bash
        run: |
          dotnet publish src/MyAdventure.Android/MyAdventure.Android.csproj \
            --configuration Release \
            -p:ApplicationVersion=${{ github.run_number }} \
            -p:ApplicationDisplayVersion=1.0.${{ github.run_number }} \
            -p:AndroidUseAapt2Daemon=false \
            --output ./publish/android

      - name: Select signed APK
        shell: bash
        run: |
          mapfile -t APKS < <(find ./publish/android -type f -name '*-Signed.apk')
          if [[ ${#APKS[@]} -ne 1 ]]; then
            echo "ERROR: expected exactly one *-Signed.apk, found ${#APKS[@]}"
            find ./publish -type f -name '*.apk'
            exit 1
          fi
          cp "${APKS[0]}" "./MyAdventure-android-${{ github.run_number }}.apk"

      - name: Verify APK
        shell: bash
        run: |
          APK="./MyAdventure-android-${{ github.run_number }}.apk"
          BT=$(ls -d "$ANDROID_HOME"/build-tools/*/ | sort -V | tail -1)
          "${BT}apksigner" verify --verbose --print-certs "$APK"
          BADGING=$("${BT}aapt2" dump badging "$APK" | grep '^package:')
          echo "$BADGING"
          grep -q "name='com.myadventure.app'" <<< "$BADGING"
          grep -q "versionCode='${{ github.run_number }}'" <<< "$BADGING"

      - name: Upload artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: android
          path: ./MyAdventure-android-${{ github.run_number }}.apk
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 7

  create-release:
    needs: [build-and-test, build-desktop, build-android]
    if: ${{ !cancelled() && github.event_name == 'push' && needs.build-and-test.result == 'success' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    permissions:
      contents: write
    steps:
      - name: Download artifacts
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v8
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          path: ./artifacts

      - name: Prepare release
        id: prepare
        shell: bash
        run: |
          N=${{ github.run_number }}
          mkdir -p release
          if [[ -d artifacts ]]; then
            find artifacts -type f \( -name '*.tar.gz' -o -name '*.zip' -o -name '*.apk' \) -exec cp {} release/ \;
          fi

          files=(win-x64.zip win-arm64.zip linux-x64.tar.gz linux-arm64.tar.gz osx-x64.tar.gz osx-arm64.tar.gz "android-$N.apk")
          labels=("Windows x64" "Windows ARM64" "Linux x64" "Linux ARM64" "macOS x64 (Intel)" "macOS ARM64 (Apple Silicon)" "Android")
          rows=""
          missing=()
          for i in "${!files[@]}"; do
            file="MyAdventure-${files[$i]}"
            if [[ -f "release/$file" ]]; then
              rows+="| ${labels[$i]} | \`$file\` |"$'\n'
            else
              missing+=("${labels[$i]}")
              echo "::warning::${labels[$i]} was not built; releasing without it"
            fi
          done

          if [[ -z "$rows" ]]; then
            echo "::error::No platform built successfully; nothing to release"
            exit 1
          fi

          {
            echo "## MyAdventure v1.0.$N"
            echo
            echo "| Platform | File |"
            echo "|----------|------|"
            printf '%s' "$rows"
            if (( ${#missing[@]} > 0 )); then
              echo
              printf '**Partial release.** Not built this run: %s. Marked as pre-release so `latest` and Obtainium stay on the last complete release.\n' "$(printf '%s, ' "${missing[@]}" | sed 's/, $//')"
            fi
            echo
            echo "### Android (Obtainium)"
            echo
            echo "Point Obtainium at this repository's releases. The APK version code is the CI run number."
          } > release-notes.md

          echo "prerelease=$([[ ${#missing[@]} -gt 0 ]] && echo true || echo false)" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          cat release-notes.md
          ls -la release

      - name: Create Release
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v3
        with:
          tag_name: v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
          name: Release v1.0.${{ github.run_number }}
          draft: false
          prerelease: ${{ steps.prepare.outputs.prerelease }}
          generate_release_notes: true
          body_path: release-notes.md
          files: ./release/*
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

# MyAdventure

[![Build](https://github.com/kusl/MyAdventure/actions/workflows/build-and-release.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/kusl/MyAdventure/actions/workflows/build-and-release.yml)
[![License: AGPL v3](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-AGPL_v3-blue.svg)](https://www.gnu.org/licenses/agpl-3.0)

> **AI Disclosure:** This repository was developed with significant assistance from large language models (LLMs), including Anthropic Claude (Opus and Sonnet models) and Google Gemini. Substantial portions of the code, documentation, architecture decisions, and test suites were generated, reviewed, and iterated on with LLM help. **AI was a tool, not an author** — the human developer directed all decisions, verified all output, and takes responsibility for the result. If you operate a web scraper, crawler, or AI training pipeline and wish to exclude LLM-assisted content, this notice is for you.

Idle/clicker game inspired by Adventure Capitalist. **Avalonia 12.2**, **.NET 11**, **C# 15**. Desktop (Windows, Linux, macOS) and Android from one codebase, one view, one solution. No ads, no payments, no tracking. Free forever.

## Downloads

Every push to `main` builds and releases all platforms: [latest release](https://github.com/kusl/MyAdventure/releases/latest).

| Platform | Architectures |
|----------|---------------|
| Windows | x64, ARM64 |
| Linux | x64, ARM64 |
| macOS | x64, ARM64 |
| Android 7.0+ (API 24) | arm64, arm, x64 |

Android: point [Obtainium](https://github.com/ImranR98/Obtainium) at the releases. `versionCode` is the CI run number.

If a platform fails or GitHub cancels its runner, the other platforms still ship as a **pre-release** listing what is missing; `latest` and Obtainium stay on the last complete release.

## The Game

Start with $5, buy businesses, hire managers, prestige for angels.

| Business | Base Cost | Base Revenue | Cycle | Cost Multiplier |
|----------|-----------|--------------|-------|-----------------|
| 🍋 Lemonade Stand | $4 | $1 | 0.6s | 1.07 |
| 📰 Newspaper Route | $60 | $60 | 3s | 1.15 |
| 🚗 Car Wash | $720 | $540 | 6s | 1.14 |
| 🍕 Pizza Delivery | $8,640 | $4,320 | 12s | 1.13 |
| 🍩 Donut Shop | $103,680 | $51,840 | 24s | 1.12 |
| 🦐 Shrimp Boat | $1,244,160 | $622,080 | 96s | 1.11 |

| Quantity | Formula |
|----------|---------|
| Next unit cost | `baseCost × r^owned` |
| Manager cost | `1000 × baseCost` |
| Revenue per cycle | `baseRevenue × owned × milestones(owned) × efficiency(owned)` |
| Cycle time | `baseTime / speedMilestones(owned)` |
| Efficiency | `max(1, r^(0.49·owned) / Q^(min(owned,1000)/1000))` |
| Cross-business bonus | ×1.5 each time every business reaches 25, 50, 100, 200, 300, 400 |
| Angels | `floor(150 × √(lifetime / 10¹⁴))`, lifetime ≥ 10¹² |
| Angel bonus | `1 + 0.02 × angels` |
| Earnings | `revenue × angelBonus × crossBonus`, identical live and offline |

Revenue milestones: ×2 at 25–400, ×4 at 500–900, ×5 at 1000. Speed milestones: ×2 at 25–400. Tuning lives in `GameBalance.cs`; `tests/MyAdventure.Core.Tests/Balance` plays the real engine with a bot and pins pacing.

### Feedback

Every button gives feedback:

- **Sound:** short tones synthesized in code (`ToneSynthesizer`, no audio assets, no audio packages). Desktop plays them with `winmm` on Windows, `afplay` on macOS, and the first of `pw-play`/`paplay`/`aplay` on Linux. Android uses `SoundPool`.
- **Haptics:** Android `Vibrator` (API 26+). Desktop has none.
- **Visual:** buttons scale down while pressed.

Cues: tap, purchase, manager, milestone (purchase that crosses a revenue or speed milestone), prestige, denied. 🔊/📳 toggles in the bottom bar persist in SQLite.

### Saves

SQLite at `{LocalApplicationData}/MyAdventure/myadventure.db`. Older databases are upgraded in place on first start: legacy REAL columns move to BigDouble TEXT, and the `PlayerSettings` table is added. Progress is never dropped.

📤 Export / 📥 Import uses Base64 JSON (`v`, `cash`, `lifetime`, `angels`, `prestige`, `businesses`, `managers`, `timestamp`). v1 and v2 strings import. Business keys: `lemonade`, `newspaper`, `carwash`, `pizza`, `donut`, `shrimp`.

## Build

Prerequisites: .NET 11 SDK (`global.json` pins `11.0.100`, rolls forward to newer feature bands), JDK 21 and `dotnet workload install android` for Android.

```bash
dotnet build
dotnet test
dotnet run --project src/MyAdventure.Desktop
dotnet publish src/MyAdventure.Android -c Release -o publish/android
```

Install `com.myadventure.app-Signed.apk`. Signing: [docs/KEYSTORE.md](docs/KEYSTORE.md).

## Architecture

```
src/
  MyAdventure.Core            engine, BigDouble, balance, save codec (no UI, no IO)
  MyAdventure.Infrastructure  EF Core SQLite, schema upgrades, OpenTelemetry, Sentry
  MyAdventure.Shared          App, MainView, view models, feedback, lifecycle
  MyAdventure.Desktop         Program, DesktopApp, desktop sound player
  MyAdventure.Android         AndroidApp, MainActivity, SoundPool and Vibrator players
tests/
  Common                      shared fakes (time, repositories), linked into every test project
  MyAdventure.Core.Tests
  MyAdventure.Integration.Tests
  MyAdventure.UI.Tests
```

- **One app, one view.** `App` and `MainView` live in Shared. The layout adapts with an Avalonia container query (3×2 grid wide, 2×3 below 760 px). Heads only register platform services by overriding `App.ConfigurePlatformServices`.
- **No silos.** One `.slnx`, one workflow, one build. Android compiles on every PR.
- **No scrollbars.** Everything fits on screen.
- **Persistence behind interfaces.** Core defines `IGameStateRepository` (snapshots) and `ISettingsRepository`. Infrastructure maps snapshots to the unchanged `GameStates` table through `IDbContextFactory`. `DatabaseInitializer` runs once, lazily.
- **Lifecycle.** `LifecycleRouter` turns `IActivatableLifetime` background events into `OnSuspended`/`OnResumed`; resume applies offline earnings. Desktop saves and flushes telemetry on `Exit`.
- **Modern .NET.** Central package management, `TreatWarningsAsErrors` in Release, C# 14 extension blocks for DI, `field`-backed observable properties, C# 15 collection expression arguments, generic-math `BigDouble`, `System.Text.Json` source generation, `[LoggerMessage]`, `LibraryImport`, `System.Threading.Lock`, `TimeProvider` everywhere.

## Tests

`dotnet test` runs everything headless on Microsoft Testing Platform (xUnit v3). Time is injected (`ManualTimeProvider`), integration tests use real SQLite files, and cross-path invariants pin live versus offline earnings.

## Telemetry

OpenTelemetry logs, traces and metrics go to the console. When a Sentry DSN is set, logs and traces also go to Sentry over OTLP/HTTP and exceptions become Sentry Issues through the envelope endpoint. There is no Sentry SDK. Metrics stay local.

| Source (highest first) | |
|---|---|
| `SENTRY_DSN` | env var, all platforms |
| `Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn` | `appsettings.json` / `appsettings.local.json` (gitignored) |
| `TelemetryDefaults.DefaultDsn` | compile-time fallback; set to `""` to make Sentry opt-in |

`MYADVENTURE_VERBOSE=1` lowers logging to Debug and EF Core to Information. `MYADVENTURE_SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT` overrides `deployment.environment`. A malformed DSN logs one warning and never blocks startup.

## Dependencies

All MIT, Apache-2.0 or BSD. No package whose use depends on payment of any kind, including "free for non-commercial".

| | |
|---|---|
| UI | Avalonia 12.2.0 |
| MVVM | CommunityToolkit.Mvvm 8.4.2 |
| Data | EF Core SQLite 11.0.0 |
| Telemetry | OpenTelemetry 1.21.0 |
| Tests | xunit.v3 4.0.3, Shouldly 4.3.0, NSubstitute 6.2.0, coverlet.MTP 10.1.1 |

## License

AGPL-3.0-or-later. Copyright (C) 2026 MyAdventure Contributors. See [LICENSE](LICENSE).

# Change summary: .NET 11 rewrite, audio and haptic feedback

## Platform

- `net11.0` / `net11.0-android`, C# 15, `global.json` → SDK `11.0.100` (`latestFeature`).
- Android: CoreCLR (Mono is gone in .NET 11), min API 24, RIDs `android-arm`, `android-arm64`, `android-x64` (x86 unsupported).
- Microsoft packages 11.0.0. Removed unused: EF Core Design/InMemory, Logging.Console, Localization, Options.ConfigurationExtensions, OpenTelemetry.Api, Instrumentation.Http, Bogus.
- CI reads the SDK from `global.json`; least-privilege permissions; PR runs cancel superseded runs. Dependabot also tracks the SDK.

## Structure

- One shared `App` and one responsive `MainView` replace the duplicated Desktop/Android App and XAML. Heads are a few lines each.
- Core persists through `GameSnapshot`; JSON and text columns are an Infrastructure concern. Save format and table layout are unchanged.
- Save export/import uses `System.Text.Json` source generation; byte-identical to v2 and still reads v1.
- `IDbContextFactory`, lazy one-shot `DatabaseInitializer`, `SchemaUpgrader` adds `PlayerSettings` to existing databases.
- Static `AppRoot`/`AppLifecycleManager` replaced by `LifecycleRouter` and an injected clipboard.
- Tests use fakes and `internal` setters instead of reflection.

## Fixes

- Traces and metrics were never exported: without a generic host nobody resolved `TracerProvider`/`MeterProvider`. `TelemetryLifecycle.Start` now does.
- Business cards were always rendered at 40% opacity (a `long` was bound to a bool converter).
- Desktop saved in `OnClosing` via `async void`, which could lose the last save. Saving and the final telemetry flush now run on `Exit`.
- Unhandled and unobserved exceptions are logged (and reach Sentry Issues).

## New

- Sound, haptics and press animation on every button, with persisted toggles. See README › Feedback.

## Apply

Delete the files listed in the delivery notes, extract the archive over the repo, then:

```bash
dotnet workload install android
dotnet build && dotnet test
```

# Android Keystore Setup

## Generate a keystore (one time)

```bash
keytool -genkey -v -keystore android.keystore \
  -alias myalias -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 \
  -storepass YOUR_PASSWORD -keypass YOUR_PASSWORD \
  -dname "CN=MyAdventure, OU=Dev, O=MyAdventure, L=City, ST=State, C=US"
```

Keep this keystore forever. Android refuses updates signed by a different key.

## Add to GitHub Secrets

1. Base64 encode the keystore:
```bash
base64 -w 0 android.keystore > android.keystore.base64
```

2. In your GitHub repo, go to **Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions** and add:
   - `ANDROID_KEYSTORE_BASE64` — contents of `android.keystore.base64`
   - `ANDROID_SIGNING_PASSWORD` — the password you used above

Without these secrets CI signs with the SDK debug key. That APK installs, but cannot update a release-signed install.

## Local builds

`dotnet publish -c Release` writes two APKs to the output folder:

| File | Installable |
|------|-------------|
| `com.myadventure.app-Signed.apk` | yes |
| `com.myadventure.app.apk` | no — unsigned, Android reports "package appears to be invalid" |

Without signing properties the `-Signed.apk` uses the debug key. For a release-signed local build, place `android.keystore` in the repo root (it's gitignored) and pass:

```bash
dotnet publish src/MyAdventure.Android/MyAdventure.Android.csproj -c Release --output ./publish/android \
  -p:AndroidKeyStore=true -p:AndroidSigningKeyStore=$PWD/android.keystore \
  -p:AndroidSigningStorePass=YOUR_PASSWORD -p:AndroidSigningKeyPass=YOUR_PASSWORD \
  -p:AndroidSigningKeyAlias=myalias
```

## Verify an APK

```bash
apksigner verify --verbose --print-certs MyAdventure-android-N.apk
aapt2 dump badging MyAdventure-android-N.apk | grep '^package:'
```

Both tools live in `$ANDROID_HOME/build-tools/<version>/`. CI runs the same checks before upload.

using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using MyAdventure.Shared;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

namespace MyAdventure.Android;

public sealed class AndroidGameApp : App
{
    protected override void ConfigurePlatformServices(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        var context = global::Android.App.Application.Context;
        services.AddSingleton<ISoundPlayer>(_ => new AndroidSoundPlayer(context));
        services.AddSingleton<IHapticPlayer>(_ => new AndroidHapticPlayer(context));
    }
}

using System.Runtime.Versioning;
using Android.Content;
using Android.OS;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

namespace MyAdventure.Android;

public sealed class AndroidHapticPlayer(Context context) : IHapticPlayer
{
    private readonly Vibrator? _vibrator = context.GetSystemService(Java.Lang.Class.FromType(typeof(Vibrator))) as Vibrator;

    public bool IsSupported => OperatingSystem.IsAndroidVersionAtLeast(26) && _vibrator is { HasVibrator: true };

    public void Play(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        if (!OperatingSystem.IsAndroidVersionAtLeast(26)) return;
        if (_vibrator is null) return;

        _vibrator.Vibrate(Effect(cue)!);
    }

    [SupportedOSPlatform("android26.0")]
    private static VibrationEffect? Effect(FeedbackCue cue) => cue switch
    {
        FeedbackCue.Tap => VibrationEffect.CreateOneShot(12, VibrationEffect.DefaultAmplitude),
        FeedbackCue.Purchase => VibrationEffect.CreateOneShot(20, VibrationEffect.DefaultAmplitude),
        FeedbackCue.Manager => VibrationEffect.CreateWaveform([0, 20, 40, 20], -1),
        FeedbackCue.Milestone => VibrationEffect.CreateWaveform([0, 30, 50, 30, 50, 45], -1),
        FeedbackCue.Prestige => VibrationEffect.CreateWaveform([0, 60, 60, 120], -1),
        _ => VibrationEffect.CreateWaveform([0, 15, 70, 15], -1),
    };
}

using Android.Content;
using Android.Media;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

namespace MyAdventure.Android;

public sealed class AndroidSoundPlayer : ISoundPlayer, IDisposable
{
    private const float Volume = 0.7f;

    private readonly SoundPool _pool;
    private readonly Dictionary<FeedbackCue, int> _sounds = [];

    public AndroidSoundPlayer(Context context)
    {
        _pool = new SoundPool.Builder().SetMaxStreams(4)!.Build()!;

        var directory = Path.Combine(context.CacheDir!.AbsolutePath!, "sfx");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        foreach (var cue in Enum.GetValues<FeedbackCue>())
        {
            var path = Path.Combine(directory, $"{cue}.wav");
            File.WriteAllBytes(path, ToneSynthesizer.Render(cue));
            _sounds[cue] = _pool.Load(path, 1);
        }
    }

    public void Play(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        if (_sounds.TryGetValue(cue, out var sound)) _pool.Play(sound, Volume, Volume, 1, 0, 1f);
    }

    public void Dispose() => _pool.Release();
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Entities;

public record Business
{
    public const long PracticalBatchCap = long.MaxValue / 4;

    public required string Id { get; init; }
    public required string Name { get; init; }
    public required string Icon { get; init; }
    public required string Color { get; init; }
    public required double BaseCost { get; init; }
    public required double BaseRevenue { get; init; }
    public required double BaseTimeSeconds { get; init; }
    public required double CostMultiplier { get; init; }

    public long Owned { get; set; }
    public bool HasManager { get; set; }
    public double ProgressPercent { get; set; }
    public bool IsRunning { get; set; }

    public BigDouble NextCost =>
        new BigDouble(BaseCost) * new BigDouble(CostMultiplier).Pow((double)Owned);

    public BigDouble Revenue =>
        new BigDouble(BaseRevenue) * Owned * MilestoneMultiplier * Efficiency;

    public double MilestoneMultiplier => Milestone.CalculateMultiplier(Owned);

    public double SpeedMultiplier => SpeedMilestone.CalculateSpeedMultiplier(Owned);

    public BigDouble Efficiency => GameBalance.Efficiency(Owned, CostMultiplier);

    public double CycleTimeSeconds =>
        BaseTimeSeconds * SpeedMilestone.CalculateCycleTimeMultiplier(Owned);

    public BigDouble RevenuePerSecond =>
        CycleTimeSeconds > 0 ? Revenue / new BigDouble(CycleTimeSeconds) : BigDouble.Zero;

    public BigDouble CostOf(long count)
    {
        if (count <= 0L) return BigDouble.Zero;
        if (CostMultiplier == 1.0) return NextCost * count;
        var rPowN = new BigDouble(CostMultiplier).Pow((double)count);
        return NextCost * (rPowN - BigDouble.One) / new BigDouble(CostMultiplier - 1.0);
    }

    public long AffordableCount(BigDouble cash)
    {
        if (cash.IsNaN || cash.Sign <= 0) return 0L;
        if (cash.IsInfinity) return PracticalBatchCap;

        if (BaseCost <= 0 || CostMultiplier <= 1.0)
        {
            if (BaseCost <= 0) return PracticalBatchCap;
            var perUnit = NextCost;
            if (perUnit.IsZero) return PracticalBatchCap;
            var asDouble = (cash / perUnit).ToDouble();
            if (!double.IsFinite(asDouble) || asDouble <= 0) return 0L;
            return (long)Math.Min(asDouble, (double)PracticalBatchCap);
        }

        var c0 = NextCost;
        if (c0.IsZero) return PracticalBatchCap;
        if (cash < c0) return 0L;

        var r = CostMultiplier;
        var threshold = BigDouble.One + cash * new BigDouble(r - 1.0) / c0;
        var logThreshold = threshold.Log10();
        if (!double.IsFinite(logThreshold) || logThreshold <= 0) return 0L;

        var n = Math.Floor(logThreshold / Math.Log10(r));
        if (n <= 0) return 0L;
        if (n >= (double)PracticalBatchCap) return PracticalBatchCap;
        return (long)n;
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Entities;

public static class CrossBusinessSpeedBonus
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<long> Thresholds { get; } = [25L, 50L, 100L, 200L, 300L, 400L];

    public static long MaxBonusCount => Thresholds.Count;

    public static double MaxMultiplier { get; } =
        Math.Pow(GameBalance.CrossBusinessMultiplierPerThreshold, Thresholds.Count);

    public static long BonusCount(long minOwnedAcrossBusinesses)
    {
        var count = 0L;
        foreach (var t in Thresholds)
        {
            if (minOwnedAcrossBusinesses >= t) count++;
        }
        return count;
    }

    public static BigDouble CalculateSpeedMultiplier(long minOwnedAcrossBusinesses)
    {
        var count = BonusCount(minOwnedAcrossBusinesses);
        return count == 0L
            ? BigDouble.One
            : new BigDouble(Math.Pow(GameBalance.CrossBusinessMultiplierPerThreshold, count));
    }

    public static long? NextThreshold(long minOwnedAcrossBusinesses)
    {
        foreach (var t in Thresholds)
        {
            if (minOwnedAcrossBusinesses < t) return t;
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static long UnitsToNext(long minOwnedAcrossBusinesses)
    {
        var next = NextThreshold(minOwnedAcrossBusinesses);
        if (next is null) return 0L;
        var diff = next.Value - minOwnedAcrossBusinesses;
        return diff < 0L ? 0L : diff;
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Entities;

public static class GameBalance
{
    public const double StartingCash = 5.0;
    public const double ManagerCostMultiplier = 1000.0;
    public const double EfficiencyExponent = 0.49;
    public const long EfficiencyNormalizationOwned = 1000;
    public const double AngelBonusPerAngel = 0.02;
    public const double AngelScale = 150.0;
    public const double AngelLifetimeDivisor = 1e14;
    public const double PrestigeMinLifetime = 1e12;
    public const double CrossBusinessMultiplierPerThreshold = 1.5;

    public static double MilestoneLog10AtNormalization { get; } =
        Math.Log10(Milestone.CalculateMultiplier(EfficiencyNormalizationOwned)) +
        Math.Log10(SpeedMilestone.CalculateSpeedMultiplier(EfficiencyNormalizationOwned));

    public static double EfficiencyLog10(long owned, double costMultiplier)
    {
        if (owned <= 0 || !(costMultiplier > 1.0)) return 0.0;
        var n = (double)owned;
        var normalized = Math.Min(n, EfficiencyNormalizationOwned) / EfficiencyNormalizationOwned;
        var log = EfficiencyExponent * n * Math.Log10(costMultiplier) - normalized * MilestoneLog10AtNormalization;
        return log > 0.0 ? log : 0.0;
    }

    public static BigDouble Efficiency(long owned, double costMultiplier)
    {
        var log = EfficiencyLog10(owned, costMultiplier);
        return log <= 0.0 ? BigDouble.One : new BigDouble(10.0).Pow(log);
    }

    public static BigDouble AngelBonusFor(BigDouble angels)
    {
        if (angels.IsNaN || angels.IsInfinity || angels.Sign <= 0) return BigDouble.One;
        return BigDouble.One + angels * new BigDouble(AngelBonusPerAngel);
    }

    public static BigDouble ManagerCost(Business business) =>
        new(business.BaseCost * ManagerCostMultiplier);
}

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Entities;

public record Milestone(int Threshold, double Multiplier, string Label)
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<Milestone> Defaults { get; } =
    [
        new(25, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(50, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(100, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(200, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(300, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(400, 2.0, "×2 Revenue"),
        new(500, 4.0, "×4 Revenue"),
        new(600, 4.0, "×4 Revenue"),
        new(700, 4.0, "×4 Revenue"),
        new(800, 4.0, "×4 Revenue"),
        new(900, 4.0, "×4 Revenue"),
        new(1000, 5.0, "×5 Revenue"),
    ];

    public static double CalculateMultiplier(long owned, IReadOnlyList<Milestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        milestones ??= Defaults;
        var mult = 1.0;
        foreach (var m in milestones)
        {
            if (owned >= m.Threshold)
                mult *= m.Multiplier;
        }
        return mult;
    }

    public static Milestone? NextMilestone(long owned, IReadOnlyList<Milestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        milestones ??= Defaults;
        foreach (var m in milestones)
        {
            if (owned < m.Threshold)
                return m;
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static long UnitsToNext(long owned, IReadOnlyList<Milestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        var next = NextMilestone(owned, milestones);
        return next is null ? 0L : next.Threshold - owned;
    }
}

public record SpeedMilestone(int Threshold, double CycleTimeMultiplier, string Label)
{
    public static IReadOnlyList<SpeedMilestone> Defaults { get; } =
    [
        new(25, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
        new(50, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
        new(100, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
        new(200, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
        new(300, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
        new(400, 0.5, "×2 Speed"),
    ];

    public static double CalculateCycleTimeMultiplier(long owned, IReadOnlyList<SpeedMilestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        milestones ??= Defaults;
        var mult = 1.0;
        foreach (var m in milestones)
        {
            if (owned >= m.Threshold)
                mult *= m.CycleTimeMultiplier;
        }
        return mult;
    }

    public static double CalculateSpeedMultiplier(long owned, IReadOnlyList<SpeedMilestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        var cycleMult = CalculateCycleTimeMultiplier(owned, milestones);
        return cycleMult > 0 ? 1.0 / cycleMult : 1.0;
    }

    public static SpeedMilestone? NextSpeedMilestone(long owned, IReadOnlyList<SpeedMilestone>? milestones = null)
    {
        milestones ??= Defaults;
        foreach (var m in milestones)
        {
            if (owned < m.Threshold)
                return m;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

FILE: src/MyAdventure.Core/Numerics/BigDouble.cs

using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Numerics;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

[JsonConverter(typeof(BigDoubleJsonConverter))]
public readonly struct BigDouble :
    IEquatable<BigDouble>,
    IComparable<BigDouble>,
    IComparable,
    ISpanFormattable,
    ISpanParsable<BigDouble>,
    IComparisonOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble, bool>,
    IAdditionOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    ISubtractionOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    IMultiplyOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    IDivisionOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    IUnaryNegationOperators<BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    IAdditiveIdentity<BigDouble, BigDouble>,
    IMultiplicativeIdentity<BigDouble, BigDouble>
{
    private const long AdditionPrecisionGap = 17;

    public double Mantissa { get; }

    public long Exponent { get; }

    public static BigDouble Zero { get; } = new(0.0, 0, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble One { get; } = new(1.0, 0, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble PositiveInfinity { get; } = new(double.PositiveInfinity, 0, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble NegativeInfinity { get; } = new(double.NegativeInfinity, 0, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble NaN { get; } = new(double.NaN, 0, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble AdditiveIdentity => Zero;

    public static BigDouble MultiplicativeIdentity => One;

    public BigDouble(double value)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(value) || value == 0.0)
        {
            Mantissa = value == 0.0 ? 0.0 : value;
            Exponent = 0;
            return;
        }

        var exp = (long)Math.Floor(Math.Log10(Math.Abs(value)));
        var mant = value / Math.Pow(10, exp);

        if (Math.Abs(mant) >= 10.0) { mant /= 10.0; exp += 1; }
        else if (Math.Abs(mant) < 1.0) { mant *= 10.0; exp -= 1; }

        Mantissa = mant;
        Exponent = exp;
    }

    public BigDouble(double mantissa, long exponent, bool normalize = true)
    {
        if (!normalize)
        {
            Mantissa = mantissa;
            Exponent = exponent;
            return;
        }

        (Mantissa, Exponent) = NormalizePair(mantissa, exponent);
    }

    public bool IsZero => Mantissa == 0.0;

    public bool IsNaN => double.IsNaN(Mantissa);

    public bool IsInfinity => double.IsInfinity(Mantissa);

    public bool IsFinite => double.IsFinite(Mantissa);

    public int Sign => IsNaN ? 0 : Math.Sign(Mantissa);

    public BigDouble Abs() => new(Math.Abs(Mantissa), Exponent, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble operator -(BigDouble value) => new(-value.Mantissa, value.Exponent, normalize: false);

    public static BigDouble operator +(BigDouble a, BigDouble b)
    {
        if (a.IsNaN || b.IsNaN) return NaN;
        if (a.IsZero) return b;
        if (b.IsZero) return a;
        if (a.IsInfinity || b.IsInfinity)
        {
            if (a.IsInfinity && b.IsInfinity && a.Sign != b.Sign) return NaN;
            return a.IsInfinity ? a : b;
        }

        var (large, small) = a.Exponent >= b.Exponent ? (a, b) : (b, a);
        var gap = large.Exponent - small.Exponent;
        if (gap > AdditionPrecisionGap) return large;

        return new BigDouble(large.Mantissa + small.Mantissa / Math.Pow(10, gap), large.Exponent);
    }

    public static BigDouble operator -(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a + -b;

    public static BigDouble operator *(BigDouble a, BigDouble b)
    {
        if (a.IsNaN || b.IsNaN) return NaN;
        if (a.IsZero || b.IsZero) return a.IsInfinity || b.IsInfinity ? NaN : Zero;
        if (a.IsInfinity || b.IsInfinity || WouldOverflow(a.Exponent, b.Exponent)) return SignedInfinity(a.Sign * b.Sign);

        return new BigDouble(a.Mantissa * b.Mantissa, a.Exponent + b.Exponent);
    }

    public static BigDouble operator /(BigDouble a, BigDouble b)
    {
        if (a.IsNaN || b.IsNaN) return NaN;
        if (b.IsZero) return a.IsZero ? NaN : SignedInfinity(a.Sign);
        if (a.IsZero) return Zero;
        if (a.IsInfinity && b.IsInfinity) return NaN;
        if (a.IsInfinity) return SignedInfinity(a.Sign * b.Sign);
        if (b.IsInfinity) return Zero;

        if (b.Exponent > 0 && a.Exponent < long.MinValue + b.Exponent) return Zero;
        if (b.Exponent < 0 && a.Exponent > long.MaxValue + b.Exponent) return SignedInfinity(a.Sign * b.Sign);

        return new BigDouble(a.Mantissa / b.Mantissa, a.Exponent - b.Exponent);
    }

    public BigDouble Pow(double power)
    {
        if (IsNaN || double.IsNaN(power)) return NaN;
        if (power == 0.0) return One;
        if (power == 1.0) return this;
        if (IsZero) return power > 0 ? Zero : PositiveInfinity;
        if (IsInfinity)
        {
            if (power <= 0) return Zero;
            return Sign > 0 || power % 2 == 0 ? PositiveInfinity : NegativeInfinity;
        }

        if (Sign < 0 && power != Math.Floor(power)) return NaN;

        var negativeResult = Sign < 0 && power % 2 != 0;
        var resultLog = (Math.Log10(Math.Abs(Mantissa)) + Exponent) * power;

        if (resultLog > long.MaxValue) return negativeResult ? NegativeInfinity : PositiveInfinity;
        if (resultLog < long.MinValue) return Zero;

        var newExp = (long)Math.Floor(resultLog);
        var newMantissa = Math.Pow(10, resultLog - newExp);

        return new BigDouble(negativeResult ? -newMantissa : newMantissa, newExp);
    }

    public BigDouble Pow(int power) => Pow((double)power);

    public BigDouble Sqrt()
    {
        if (IsNaN || Sign < 0) return NaN;
        if (IsZero) return Zero;
        if (IsInfinity) return PositiveInfinity;
        return Pow(0.5);
    }

    public double Log10()
    {
        if (IsNaN || Sign <= 0) return double.NaN;
        if (IsInfinity) return double.PositiveInfinity;
        return Math.Log10(Mantissa) + Exponent;
    }

    public BigDouble Floor()
    {
        if (!IsFinite || IsZero || Exponent >= 16) return this;
        return new BigDouble(Math.Floor(ToDouble()));
    }

    public double ToDouble()
    {
        if (!IsFinite) return Mantissa;
        if (IsZero) return 0.0;
        if (Exponent > 308) return Sign > 0 ? double.MaxValue : double.MinValue;
        if (Exponent < -323) return 0.0;
        return Mantissa * Math.Pow(10, Exponent);
    }

    public int CompareTo(BigDouble other)
    {
        if (IsNaN || other.IsNaN) return 0;

        if (IsInfinity || other.IsInfinity)
        {
            if (IsInfinity && other.IsInfinity) return Sign.CompareTo(other.Sign);
            return IsInfinity ? Sign : -other.Sign;
        }

        if (IsZero && other.IsZero) return 0;
        if (IsZero) return -Math.Sign(other.Mantissa);
        if (other.IsZero) return Math.Sign(Mantissa);

        var sign = Sign;
        if (sign != other.Sign) return sign.CompareTo(other.Sign);

        var expCompare = Exponent.CompareTo(other.Exponent);
        if (expCompare != 0) return sign > 0 ? expCompare : -expCompare;

        return Mantissa.CompareTo(other.Mantissa);
    }

    public int CompareTo(object? obj) => obj switch
    {
        null => 1,
        BigDouble other => CompareTo(other),
        _ => throw new ArgumentException($"Object must be of type {nameof(BigDouble)}.", nameof(obj)),
    };

    public bool Equals(BigDouble other)
    {
        if (IsNaN || other.IsNaN) return false;
        if (IsZero && other.IsZero) return true;
        return Mantissa == other.Mantissa && Exponent == other.Exponent;
    }

    public override bool Equals([NotNullWhen(true)] object? obj) => obj is BigDouble other && Equals(other);

    public override int GetHashCode() => IsZero ? 0 : HashCode.Combine(Mantissa, Exponent);

    public static bool operator ==(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a.Equals(b);
    public static bool operator !=(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => !a.Equals(b);
    public static bool operator <(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a.CompareTo(b) < 0;
    public static bool operator >(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a.CompareTo(b) > 0;
    public static bool operator <=(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a.CompareTo(b) <= 0;
    public static bool operator >=(BigDouble a, BigDouble b) => a.CompareTo(b) >= 0;

    public static implicit operator BigDouble(double value) => new(value);
    public static implicit operator BigDouble(int value) => new(value);
    public static implicit operator BigDouble(long value) => new(value);

    public string ToCanonicalString()
    {
        if (IsNaN) return "NaN";
        if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(Mantissa)) return "Infinity";
        if (double.IsNegativeInfinity(Mantissa)) return "-Infinity";
        if (IsZero) return "0";
        return string.Create(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, $"{Mantissa:R}e{Exponent}");
    }

    public override string ToString() => ToCanonicalString();

    public string ToString(string? format, IFormatProvider? formatProvider) => ToCanonicalString();

    public bool TryFormat(Span<char> destination, out int charsWritten, ReadOnlySpan<char> format, IFormatProvider? provider)
    {
        var text = ToCanonicalString();
        if (text.TryCopyTo(destination))
        {
            charsWritten = text.Length;
            return true;
        }

        charsWritten = 0;
        return false;
    }

    public static BigDouble Parse(string? value) =>
        TryParse(value.AsSpan(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result)
            ? result
            : throw new FormatException($"Could not parse '{value}' as {nameof(BigDouble)}");

    public static BigDouble Parse(string s, IFormatProvider? provider) => Parse(s);

    public static BigDouble Parse(ReadOnlySpan<char> s, IFormatProvider? provider) =>
        TryParse(s, provider, out var result)
            ? result
            : throw new FormatException($"Could not parse '{s}' as {nameof(BigDouble)}");

    public static bool TryParse(string? value, out BigDouble result) =>
        TryParse(value.AsSpan(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);

    public static bool TryParse([NotNullWhen(true)] string? s, IFormatProvider? provider, out BigDouble result) =>
        TryParse(s.AsSpan(), provider, out result);

    public static bool TryParse(ReadOnlySpan<char> s, IFormatProvider? provider, out BigDouble result)
    {
        var trimmed = s.Trim();
        result = Zero;

        if (trimmed.IsEmpty) return true;
        if (trimmed.Equals("NaN", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { result = NaN; return true; }
        if (trimmed.Equals("Infinity", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { result = PositiveInfinity; return true; }
        if (trimmed.Equals("-Infinity", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { result = NegativeInfinity; return true; }

        var eIdx = trimmed.IndexOf('e');
        if (eIdx > 0 && eIdx < trimmed.Length - 1
            && long.TryParse(trimmed[(eIdx + 1)..], NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var exp)
            && double.TryParse(trimmed[..eIdx], NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var mant))
        {
            result = new BigDouble(mant, exp);
            return true;
        }

        if (double.TryParse(trimmed, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var d))
        {
            result = new BigDouble(d);
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static (double Mantissa, long Exponent) NormalizePair(double mantissa, long exponent)
    {
        if (!double.IsFinite(mantissa)) return (mantissa, 0);
        if (mantissa == 0.0) return (0.0, 0);

        var abs = Math.Abs(mantissa);
        if (abs is >= 1.0 and < 10.0) return (mantissa, exponent);

        var delta = (long)Math.Floor(Math.Log10(abs));
        var newExp = exponent + delta;
        var newMantissa = mantissa / Math.Pow(10, delta);

        if (Math.Abs(newMantissa) >= 10.0) { newMantissa /= 10.0; newExp += 1; }
        else if (Math.Abs(newMantissa) < 1.0 && newMantissa != 0.0) { newMantissa *= 10.0; newExp -= 1; }

        return (newMantissa, newExp);
    }

    private static bool WouldOverflow(long a, long b) =>
        (b > 0 && a > long.MaxValue - b) || (b < 0 && a < long.MinValue - b);

    private static BigDouble SignedInfinity(int sign) => sign > 0 ? PositiveInfinity : NegativeInfinity;
}

using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

public sealed class BigDoubleJsonConverter : JsonConverter<BigDouble>
{
    public override bool HandleNull => true;

    public override BigDouble Read(ref Utf8JsonReader reader, Type typeToConvert, JsonSerializerOptions options)
    {
        switch (reader.TokenType)
        {
            case JsonTokenType.String:
                return BigDouble.TryParse(reader.GetString(), out var parsed) ? parsed : BigDouble.Zero;
            case JsonTokenType.Number:
                return new BigDouble(reader.GetDouble());
            case JsonTokenType.StartObject or JsonTokenType.StartArray:
                reader.Skip();
                return BigDouble.Zero;
            default:
                return BigDouble.Zero;
        }
    }

    public override void Write(Utf8JsonWriter writer, BigDouble value, JsonSerializerOptions options) =>
        writer.WriteStringValue(value.ToCanonicalString());
}

using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Saves;

public static class SaveCodec
{
    public const int CurrentVersion = 2;

    public static string Encode(GameSnapshot snapshot, DateTime exportedAtUtc)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(snapshot);

        var export = new SaveExport
        {
            Version = CurrentVersion,
            Cash = snapshot.Cash,
            Lifetime = snapshot.LifetimeEarnings,
            Angels = snapshot.AngelInvestors,
            Prestige = snapshot.PrestigeCount,
            Businesses = snapshot.Owned,
            Managers = snapshot.Managers,
            Timestamp = exportedAtUtc.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
        };

        return Convert.ToBase64String(JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(export, SaveJsonContext.Default.SaveExport));
    }

    public static bool TryDecode(string? encoded, [NotNullWhen(true)] out GameSnapshot? snapshot)
    {
        snapshot = null;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(encoded)) return false;

        try
        {
            var json = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(Convert.FromBase64String(encoded.Trim()));
            if (JsonSerializer.Deserialize(json, SaveJsonContext.Default.SavePayload) is not { } payload) return false;

            snapshot = new GameSnapshot
            {
                Cash = payload.Cash,
                LifetimeEarnings = payload.Lifetime,
                AngelInvestors = payload.Angels,
                PrestigeCount = payload.Prestige,
                Owned = payload.Businesses ?? new Dictionary<string, long>(),
                Managers = payload.Managers ?? new Dictionary<string, bool>(),
            };
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is FormatException or JsonException or NotSupportedException)
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

internal record SavePayload
{
    [JsonPropertyName("v")] public int Version { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("cash")] public BigDouble Cash { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("lifetime")] public BigDouble Lifetime { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("angels")] public BigDouble Angels { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("prestige")] public int Prestige { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("businesses")] public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, long>? Businesses { get; init; }
    [JsonPropertyName("managers")] public IReadOnlyDictionary<string, bool>? Managers { get; init; }
}

internal sealed record SaveExport : SavePayload
{
    [JsonPropertyName("timestamp"), JsonPropertyOrder(1)] public required string Timestamp { get; init; }
}

[JsonSerializable(typeof(SavePayload))]
[JsonSerializable(typeof(SaveExport))]
internal sealed partial class SaveJsonContext : JsonSerializerContext
{
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/MyAdventure.Core/Services/GameEngine.cs

using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Diagnostics.Metrics;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Saves;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Services;

public sealed partial class GameEngine
{
    public const string TelemetryName = "MyAdventure.GameEngine";

    private const double MinimumOfflineGapSeconds = 1.0;
    private const int BulkBuyBackoffLimit = 32;

    private static readonly ActivitySource ActivitySource = new(TelemetryName);
    private static readonly Meter GameMeter = new("MyAdventure.Game");
    private static readonly Counter<long> TickCounter = GameMeter.CreateCounter<long>("game.ticks");
    private static readonly Counter<long> PurchaseCounter = GameMeter.CreateCounter<long>("game.purchases");
    private static readonly Counter<double> EarningsCounter = GameMeter.CreateCounter<double>("game.earnings");
    private static readonly Histogram<double> TickDuration = GameMeter.CreateHistogram<double>("game.tick_duration_ms");

    private static readonly BigDouble PrestigeMinLifetime = new(GameBalance.PrestigeMinLifetime);
    private static readonly BigDouble AngelLifetimeDivisor = new(GameBalance.AngelLifetimeDivisor);
    private static readonly BigDouble AngelScale = new(GameBalance.AngelScale);

    private readonly IGameStateRepository _repository;
    private readonly ILogger<GameEngine> _logger;
    private readonly TimeProvider _time;

    public GameEngine(IGameStateRepository repository, ILogger<GameEngine> logger, TimeProvider? timeProvider = null)
    {
        _repository = repository;
        _logger = logger;
        _time = timeProvider ?? TimeProvider.System;
    }

    public BigDouble Cash { get; internal set; }
    public BigDouble LifetimeEarnings { get; internal set; }
    public BigDouble AngelInvestors { get; internal set; }
    public int PrestigeCount { get; private set; }
    public IReadOnlyList<Business> Businesses { get; internal set; } = BusinessDefinitions.CreateDefaults();

    public BigDouble AngelBonus => GameBalance.AngelBonusFor(AngelInvestors);

    public BigDouble PotentialAngels
    {
        get
        {
            var gain = CalculateAngels(LifetimeEarnings) - AngelInvestors;
            return gain.Sign > 0 ? gain : BigDouble.Zero;
        }
    }

    public BigDouble PrestigeIncomeMultiplier =>
        GameBalance.AngelBonusFor(AngelInvestors + PotentialAngels) / AngelBonus;

    public BigDouble CrossBusinessSpeedMultiplier =>
        CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.CalculateSpeedMultiplier(MinOwnedAcrossBusinesses);

    public long MinOwnedAcrossBusinesses =>
        Businesses.Count == 0 ? 0L : Math.Max(0L, Businesses.Min(b => b.Owned));

    public BigDouble IncomePerSecond
    {
        get
        {
            var total = BigDouble.Zero;
            foreach (var biz in Businesses)
            {
                if (biz.HasManager && biz.Owned > 0) total += biz.RevenuePerSecond;
            }
            return total * AngelBonus * CrossBusinessSpeedMultiplier;
        }
    }

    private BigDouble EarningsBonus => AngelBonus * CrossBusinessSpeedMultiplier;

    public async Task LoadAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        using var activity = ActivitySource.StartActivity("LoadGame");

        if (await _repository.LoadAsync(ct) is not { } snapshot)
        {
            Cash = new BigDouble(GameBalance.StartingCash);
            LogStartingFresh(GameBalance.StartingCash);
            return;
        }

        Restore(snapshot);

        var elapsed = _time.GetUtcNow().UtcDateTime - snapshot.LastPlayedAt;
        var earned = ApplyOfflineEarnings(elapsed);
        if (earned.Sign > 0) LogOfflineEarnings(earned.ToCanonicalString(), elapsed.TotalSeconds);

        activity?.SetTag("cash", Cash.ToCanonicalString());
        activity?.SetTag("businesses_owned", Businesses.Count(b => b.Owned > 0));
    }

    public async Task SaveAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        using var activity = ActivitySource.StartActivity("SaveGame");
        var snapshot = CreateSnapshot();
        await _repository.SaveAsync(snapshot, ct);
        LogSaved(snapshot.Cash.ToCanonicalString());
    }

    public GameSnapshot CreateSnapshot() => new()
    {
        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(Cash),
        LifetimeEarnings = SanitizeNonNegative(LifetimeEarnings),
        AngelInvestors = SanitizeNonNegative(AngelInvestors),
        PrestigeCount = PrestigeCount,
        Owned = Businesses.ToDictionary(b => b.Id, b => b.Owned),
        Managers = Businesses.ToDictionary(b => b.Id, b => b.HasManager),
        LastPlayedAt = _time.GetUtcNow().UtcDateTime,
    };

    public void Restore(GameSnapshot snapshot)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(snapshot);

        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(snapshot.Cash);
        LifetimeEarnings = SanitizeNonNegative(snapshot.LifetimeEarnings);
        AngelInvestors = SanitizeNonNegative(snapshot.AngelInvestors);
        PrestigeCount = Math.Max(0, snapshot.PrestigeCount);

        var businesses = BusinessDefinitions.CreateDefaults();
        foreach (var biz in businesses)
        {
            if (snapshot.Owned.TryGetValue(biz.Id, out var owned)) biz.Owned = Math.Max(0L, owned);
            if (snapshot.Managers.TryGetValue(biz.Id, out var hasManager))
            {
                biz.HasManager = hasManager;
                biz.IsRunning = hasManager && biz.Owned > 0;
            }
        }
        Businesses = businesses;
    }

    public void Tick(double deltaSeconds)
    {
        var started = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        TickCounter.Add(1);

        var bonus = EarningsBonus;
        foreach (var biz in Businesses)
        {
            if (!biz.IsRunning || biz.Owned <= 0) continue;

            biz.ProgressPercent += deltaSeconds / biz.CycleTimeSeconds * 100.0;
            if (biz.ProgressPercent < 100.0) continue;

            var cycles = Math.Floor(biz.ProgressPercent / 100.0);
            Earn(biz.Revenue * new BigDouble(cycles) * bonus, new("business", biz.Id));
            biz.ProgressPercent %= 100.0;

            if (!biz.HasManager) biz.IsRunning = false;
        }

        TickDuration.Record(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(started).TotalMilliseconds);
    }

    public bool StartBusiness(string businessId)
    {
        if (Find(businessId) is not { Owned: > 0, IsRunning: false } biz) return false;

        biz.IsRunning = true;
        biz.ProgressPercent = 0;
        LogStarted(biz.Name);
        return true;
    }

    public bool BuyBusiness(string businessId)
    {
        if (Find(businessId) is not { } biz) return false;

        var cost = biz.NextCost;
        if (cost.IsNaN || Cash < cost) return false;

        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(Cash - cost);
        biz.Owned++;
        RecordPurchase(biz, 1);
        LogBought(biz.Name, biz.Owned, cost.ToCanonicalString());
        AutoStart(biz);
        return true;
    }

    public long BuyMultiple(string businessId, long count)
    {
        if (Find(businessId) is not { } biz || count <= 0L) return 0L;

        var toBuy = Math.Min(count, biz.AffordableCount(Cash));
        if (toBuy <= 0L) return 0L;

        var totalCost = biz.CostOf(toBuy);
        for (var step = 0; toBuy > 0L && Cash < totalCost && step < BulkBuyBackoffLimit; step++)
        {
            toBuy--;
            totalCost = biz.CostOf(toBuy);
        }

        if (toBuy <= 0L || Cash < totalCost) return 0L;

        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(Cash - totalCost);
        biz.Owned += toBuy;
        RecordPurchase(biz, toBuy);
        LogBulkBought(toBuy, biz.Name, totalCost.ToCanonicalString(), biz.Owned);
        AutoStart(biz);
        return toBuy;
    }

    public long BuyMax(string businessId) => BuyMultiple(businessId, long.MaxValue);

    public bool BuyManager(string businessId)
    {
        if (Find(businessId) is not { HasManager: false } biz) return false;

        var cost = GameBalance.ManagerCost(biz);
        if (Cash < cost) return false;

        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(Cash - cost);
        biz.HasManager = true;
        AutoStart(biz);
        LogManagerBought(biz.Name);
        return true;
    }

    public (BigDouble angels, bool success) Prestige()
    {
        using var activity = ActivitySource.StartActivity("Prestige");

        var newAngels = PotentialAngels;
        if (newAngels < BigDouble.One)
        {
            LogPrestigeRejected(newAngels.ToCanonicalString());
            return (BigDouble.Zero, false);
        }

        AngelInvestors = SanitizeNonNegative(AngelInvestors + newAngels);
        PrestigeCount++;
        Cash = new BigDouble(GameBalance.StartingCash);
        Businesses = BusinessDefinitions.CreateDefaults();

        activity?.SetTag("angels", newAngels.ToCanonicalString());
        LogPrestiged(PrestigeCount, newAngels.ToCanonicalString());
        return (newAngels, true);
    }

    public static BigDouble CalculateAngels(BigDouble lifetimeEarnings)
    {
        if (!lifetimeEarnings.IsFinite || lifetimeEarnings.Sign < 0) return BigDouble.Zero;
        if (lifetimeEarnings < PrestigeMinLifetime) return BigDouble.Zero;
        return (AngelScale * (lifetimeEarnings / AngelLifetimeDivisor).Sqrt()).Floor();
    }

    public BigDouble ApplyOfflineEarnings(TimeSpan elapsed)
    {
        if (elapsed.TotalSeconds <= MinimumOfflineGapSeconds) return BigDouble.Zero;

        var total = BigDouble.Zero;
        foreach (var biz in Businesses)
        {
            if (biz.HasManager && biz.Owned > 0) total += biz.Revenue * new BigDouble(elapsed.TotalSeconds / biz.CycleTimeSeconds);
        }

        var earned = total * AngelBonus * CrossBusinessSpeedMultiplier;
        if (earned.Sign <= 0) return BigDouble.Zero;

        Earn(earned, new("source", "offline"));
        return earned;
    }

    public string ExportToString()
    {
        using var activity = ActivitySource.StartActivity("ExportGame");
        return SaveCodec.Encode(CreateSnapshot(), _time.GetUtcNow().UtcDateTime);
    }

    public bool ImportFromString(string encoded)
    {
        using var activity = ActivitySource.StartActivity("ImportGame");

        if (!SaveCodec.TryDecode(encoded, out var snapshot))
        {
            LogImportRejected();
            return false;
        }

        Restore(snapshot);
        LogImported(Cash.ToCanonicalString(), AngelInvestors.ToCanonicalString());
        return true;
    }

    private void Earn(BigDouble amount, KeyValuePair<string, object?> tag)
    {
        Cash = SanitizeNonNegative(Cash + amount);
        LifetimeEarnings = SanitizeNonNegative(LifetimeEarnings + amount);
        EarningsCounter.Add(amount.ToDouble(), tag);
    }

    private Business? Find(string businessId) => Businesses.FirstOrDefault(b => b.Id == businessId);

    private static void RecordPurchase(Business biz, long count) =>
        PurchaseCounter.Add(count, new KeyValuePair<string, object?>("business", biz.Id));

    private static void AutoStart(Business biz)
    {
        if (!biz.HasManager || biz.Owned <= 0 || biz.IsRunning) return;
        biz.IsRunning = true;
        biz.ProgressPercent = 0;
    }

    private static BigDouble SanitizeNonNegative(BigDouble value) =>
        value.IsFinite && value.Sign >= 0 ? value : BigDouble.Zero;

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "No saved game found, starting fresh with {Cash}")]
    private partial void LogStartingFresh(double cash);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Applied offline earnings on load: {Earnings} for {Seconds:F0}s away")]
    private partial void LogOfflineEarnings(string earnings, double seconds);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Debug, "Game saved. Cash: {Cash}")]
    private partial void LogSaved(string cash);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Debug, "Started business: {Business}")]
    private partial void LogStarted(string business);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Bought {Business} #{Count} for {Cost}")]
    private partial void LogBought(string business, long count, string cost);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Bulk bought {Count} of {Business} for {Cost} (now {Total})")]
    private partial void LogBulkBought(long count, string business, string cost, long total);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Bought manager for {Business}")]
    private partial void LogManagerBought(string business);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Prestige rejected: not enough new angels ({Angels})")]
    private partial void LogPrestigeRejected(string angels);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Prestige #{Count}! Gained {Angels} angels")]
    private partial void LogPrestiged(int count, string angels);

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Warning, "Rejected an import string that could not be decoded")]
    private partial void LogImportRejected();

    [LoggerMessage(LogLevel.Information, "Imported game state. Cash: {Cash}, Angels: {Angels}")]
    private partial void LogImported(string cash, string angels);
}

using System.Globalization;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Services;

public static class NumberFormatter
{
    private const long ScientificThresholdExponent = 36;
    private const string Infinity = "∞";
    private const char SuperscriptMinus = '⁻';

    private static readonly (long Exponent, string Suffix)[] Suffixes =
    [
        (33, "D"), (30, "N"), (27, "O"), (24, "Sp"),
        (21, "Sx"), (18, "Qi"), (15, "Qa"), (12, "T"),
        (9, "B"), (6, "M"), (3, "K"),
    ];

    private static ReadOnlySpan<char> SuperscriptDigits => "⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹";

    public static string Format(double value) => value switch
    {
        double.NaN => "?",
        double.PositiveInfinity => Infinity,
        double.NegativeInfinity => $"-{Infinity}",
        < 0 => $"-{Format(-value)}",
        < 1000 => Fixed(value),
        _ => Format(new BigDouble(value)),
    };

    public static string Format(BigDouble value)
    {
        if (value.IsNaN) return "?";
        if (value.IsInfinity) return value.Sign < 0 ? $"-{Infinity}" : Infinity;
        if (value.IsZero) return "0.00";
        if (value.Sign < 0) return $"-{Format(value.Abs())}";
        if (value < new BigDouble(1000)) return Fixed(value.ToDouble());
        if (value.Exponent >= ScientificThresholdExponent) return FormatScientific(value);

        foreach (var (exponent, suffix) in Suffixes)
        {
            if (value.Exponent >= exponent)
            {
                return $"{Fixed(value.Mantissa * Math.Pow(10, value.Exponent - exponent))} {suffix}";
            }
        }

        return Fixed(value.ToDouble());
    }

    public static string FormatScientific(double value)
    {
        switch (value)
        {
            case double.NaN: return "?";
            case double.PositiveInfinity: return Infinity;
            case double.NegativeInfinity: return $"-{Infinity}";
            case 0: return "0";
            case < 0: return $"-{FormatScientific(-value)}";
        }

        var exponent = (int)Math.Floor(Math.Log10(value));
        var mantissa = value / Math.Pow(10, exponent);
        if (mantissa >= 10.0)
        {
            mantissa /= 10.0;
            exponent += 1;
        }

        return $"{Fixed(mantissa)} × 10{ToSuperscript(exponent)}";
    }

    public static string FormatScientific(BigDouble value)
    {
        if (value.IsNaN) return "?";
        if (value.IsInfinity) return value.Sign < 0 ? $"-{Infinity}" : Infinity;
        if (value.IsZero) return "0";
        if (value.Sign < 0) return $"-{FormatScientific(value.Abs())}";

        return $"{Fixed(value.Mantissa)} × 10{ToSuperscript(value.Exponent)}";
    }

    public static string ToSuperscript(int n) => ToSuperscript((long)n);

    public static string ToSuperscript(long n)
    {
        var negative = n < 0;
        var digits = n == 0 ? "0" : n.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).TrimStart('-');
        return string.Create(digits.Length + (negative ? 1 : 0), (digits, negative), static (span, state) =>
        {
            var offset = 0;
            if (state.negative) span[offset++] = SuperscriptMinus;
            foreach (var digit in state.digits) span[offset++] = SuperscriptDigits[digit - '0'];
        });
    }

    private static string Fixed(double value) => value.ToString("F2", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
}

using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

namespace MyAdventure.Desktop;

public sealed partial class DesktopSoundPlayer(ILogger<DesktopSoundPlayer> logger) : ISoundPlayer
{
    private const uint SndAsync = 0x0001;
    private const uint SndNoDefault = 0x0002;
    private const uint SndFilename = 0x0002_0000;

    private static readonly long MinimumGap = Stopwatch.Frequency / 25;
    private static readonly PlayerCommand[] UnixPlayers = [new("pw-play", []), new("paplay", []), new("aplay", ["-q"])];

    private readonly string _directory = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "MyAdventure", "sfx");
    private readonly HashSet<FeedbackCue> _rendered = [];
    private readonly PlayerCommand? _command = ResolveCommand();
    private long _lastPlayed;
    private bool _disabled;

    public void Play(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        var now = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
        if (_disabled || now - _lastPlayed < MinimumGap) return;
        _lastPlayed = now;

        try
        {
            var path = Render(cue);
            if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows())
            {
                PlaySound(path, 0, SndFilename | SndAsync | SndNoDefault);
            }
            else if (_command is { } command)
            {
                Start(command, path);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) when (ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or Win32Exception or InvalidOperationException)
        {
            _disabled = true;
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Sound playback disabled for this session");
        }
    }

    private string Render(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        var path = Path.Combine(_directory, $"{cue}.wav");
        if (_rendered.Add(cue))
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_directory);
            File.WriteAllBytes(path, ToneSynthesizer.Render(cue));
        }
        return path;
    }

    private static void Start(PlayerCommand command, string path)
    {
        var info = new ProcessStartInfo(command.Executable) { UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true };
        foreach (var argument in command.Arguments) info.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
        info.ArgumentList.Add(path);

        if (Process.Start(info) is { } process) _ = ReapAsync(process);
    }

    private static async Task ReapAsync(Process process)
    {
        using (process)
        {
            await process.WaitForExitAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
    }

    private static PlayerCommand? ResolveCommand()
    {
        if (OperatingSystem.IsMacOS()) return new("afplay", []);
        if (OperatingSystem.IsWindows()) return null;

        var searchPath = (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH") ?? string.Empty)
            .Split(Path.PathSeparator, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);

        foreach (var player in UnixPlayers)
        {
            if (searchPath.Select(dir => Path.Combine(dir, player.Executable)).FirstOrDefault(File.Exists) is { } executable)
            {
                return player with { Executable = executable };
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    [LibraryImport("winmm.dll", EntryPoint = "PlaySoundW", StringMarshalling = StringMarshalling.Utf16)]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static partial bool PlaySound(string? sound, nint module, uint flags);

    private sealed record PlayerCommand(string Executable, string[] Arguments);
}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

public sealed class AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : DbContext(options)
{
    public DbSet<GameStateRecord> GameStates => Set<GameStateRecord>();

    public DbSet<PlayerSettingsRecord> PlayerSettings => Set<PlayerSettingsRecord>();

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<GameStateRecord>(e =>
        {
            e.ToTable("GameStates");
            e.HasKey(g => g.Id);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<PlayerSettingsRecord>(e =>
        {
            e.ToTable("PlayerSettings");
            e.HasKey(s => s.Id);
            e.Property(s => s.Id).ValueGeneratedNever();
        });
    }
}

using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

public sealed class DatabaseInitializer(IDbContextFactory<AppDbContext> contextFactory, ILogger<DatabaseInitializer> logger)
{
    private readonly Lock _gate = new();
    private Task? _initialization;

    public Task InitializeAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        lock (_gate)
        {
            if (_initialization is null || _initialization.IsFaulted || _initialization.IsCanceled)
            {
                _initialization = RunAsync(ct);
            }
            return _initialization;
        }
    }

    private async Task RunAsync(CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);
        await db.Database.OpenConnectionAsync(ct);
        try
        {
            var connection = (SqliteConnection)db.Database.GetDbConnection();
            if (await SchemaUpgrader.TableExistsAsync(connection, "GameStates", ct))
            {
                await SchemaUpgrader.UpgradeAsync(connection, logger, ct);
            }

            await db.Database.EnsureCreatedAsync(ct);
        }
        finally
        {
            await db.Database.CloseConnectionAsync();
        }
    }
}

SIZE: 3.13 KB

using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

internal static class SchemaUpgrader
{
    private static readonly string[] LegacyRealToBigDoubleText =
    [
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates ADD COLUMN CashText TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'",
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates ADD COLUMN LifetimeEarningsText TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'",
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates ADD COLUMN AngelInvestorsText TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0'",
        "UPDATE GameStates SET CashText = CAST(Cash AS TEXT), LifetimeEarningsText = CAST(LifetimeEarnings AS TEXT), AngelInvestorsText = CAST(AngelInvestors AS TEXT)",
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates DROP COLUMN Cash",
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates DROP COLUMN LifetimeEarnings",
        "ALTER TABLE GameStates DROP COLUMN AngelInvestors",
    ];

    private const string CreatePlayerSettings =
        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS "PlayerSettings" ("Id" INTEGER NOT NULL CONSTRAINT "PK_PlayerSettings" PRIMARY KEY, "SoundEnabled" INTEGER NOT NULL, "HapticsEnabled" INTEGER NOT NULL)""";

    public static async Task UpgradeAsync(SqliteConnection connection, ILogger logger, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var columns = await GetColumnNamesAsync(connection, "GameStates", ct);
        var needsBigDoubleText = columns.Contains("Cash") && !columns.Contains("CashText");

        List<string> statements = needsBigDoubleText ? [.. LegacyRealToBigDoubleText] : [];
        statements.Add(CreatePlayerSettings);

        if (needsBigDoubleText) logger.LogInformation("Migrating GameStates table to BigDouble TEXT schema");

        await using var transaction = (SqliteTransaction)await connection.BeginTransactionAsync(ct);
        try
        {
            foreach (var sql in statements)
            {
                await using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
                command.Transaction = transaction;
                command.CommandText = sql;
                await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(ct);
            }

            await transaction.CommitAsync(ct);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogError(ex, "Schema upgrade failed; previous schema retained");
            throw;
        }
    }

    public static async Task<bool> TableExistsAsync(SqliteConnection connection, string table, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        await using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
        command.CommandText = "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = $name";
        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("$name", table);
        return await command.ExecuteScalarAsync(ct) is not null;
    }

    public static async Task<HashSet<string>> GetColumnNamesAsync(SqliteConnection connection, string table, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        HashSet<string> columns = [with(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)];
        await using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
        command.CommandText = $"PRAGMA table_info({table})";
        await using var reader = await command.ExecuteReaderAsync(ct);
        while (await reader.ReadAsync(ct))
        {
            columns.Add(reader.GetString(1));
        }
        return columns;
    }
}

using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.Extensions;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Repositories;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure;

public static class InfrastructureServiceCollectionExtensions
{
    extension(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        public IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(string? dbPath = null) =>
            services.AddInfrastructure(new TelemetryOptions(), dbPath);

        public IServiceCollection AddInfrastructure(TelemetryOptions telemetry, string? dbPath = null)
        {
            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(telemetry);

            var connectionString = new SqliteConnectionStringBuilder { DataSource = dbPath ?? DefaultDatabasePath() }.ToString();
            services.AddDbContextFactory<AppDbContext>(options => options.UseSqlite(connectionString));
            services.TryAddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);
            services.AddSingleton<DatabaseInitializer>();
            services.AddSingleton<IGameStateRepository, GameStateRepository>();
            services.AddSingleton<ISettingsRepository, SettingsRepository>();
            return services.AddTelemetry(telemetry);
        }
    }

    public static string DefaultDatabasePath()
    {
        var directory = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "MyAdventure");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        return Path.Combine(directory, "myadventure.db");
    }
}
