using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization.Metadata;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Saves;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Repositories;

public sealed class GameStateRepository(
    IDbContextFactory<AppDbContext> contextFactory,
    DatabaseInitializer initializer,
    TimeProvider time,
    ILogger<GameStateRepository> logger) : IGameStateRepository
{
    public async Task<GameSnapshot?> LoadAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        await initializer.InitializeAsync(ct);
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);
        var record = await db.GameStates.AsNoTracking().OrderByDescending(g => g.UpdatedAt).FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
        return record is null ? null : ToSnapshot(record);
    }

    public async Task SaveAsync(GameSnapshot snapshot, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(snapshot);

        await initializer.InitializeAsync(ct);
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);

        var now = time.GetUtcNow().UtcDateTime;
        var record = await db.GameStates.OrderByDescending(g => g.UpdatedAt).FirstOrDefaultAsync(ct);
        if (record is null)
        {
            record = new GameStateRecord { CreatedAt = now };
            db.GameStates.Add(record);
        }

        record.CashText = snapshot.Cash.ToCanonicalString();
        record.LifetimeEarningsText = snapshot.LifetimeEarnings.ToCanonicalString();
        record.AngelInvestorsText = snapshot.AngelInvestors.ToCanonicalString();
        record.PrestigeCount = snapshot.PrestigeCount;
        record.BusinessDataJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new Dictionary<string, long>(snapshot.Owned), OwnershipJsonContext.Default.DictionaryStringInt64);
        record.ManagerDataJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(new Dictionary<string, bool>(snapshot.Managers), OwnershipJsonContext.Default.DictionaryStringBoolean);
        record.LastPlayedAt = snapshot.LastPlayedAt;
        record.UpdatedAt = now;

        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
        logger.LogDebug("Game state saved");
    }

    public async Task DeleteAllAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        await initializer.InitializeAsync(ct);
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);
        await db.GameStates.ExecuteDeleteAsync(ct);
        logger.LogInformation("All game states deleted");
    }

    private GameSnapshot ToSnapshot(GameStateRecord record) => new()
    {
        Cash = ParseAmount(record.CashText),
        LifetimeEarnings = ParseAmount(record.LifetimeEarningsText),
        AngelInvestors = ParseAmount(record.AngelInvestorsText),
        PrestigeCount = record.PrestigeCount,
        Owned = ParseMap(record.BusinessDataJson, OwnershipJsonContext.Default.DictionaryStringInt64),
        Managers = ParseMap(record.ManagerDataJson, OwnershipJsonContext.Default.DictionaryStringBoolean),
        LastPlayedAt = DateTime.SpecifyKind(record.LastPlayedAt, DateTimeKind.Utc),
    };

    private static BigDouble ParseAmount(string text) =>
        BigDouble.TryParse(text, out var value) ? value : BigDouble.Zero;

    private Dictionary<string, T> ParseMap<T>(string json, JsonTypeInfo<Dictionary<string, T>> typeInfo)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(json)) return [];

        try
        {
            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize(json, typeInfo) ?? [];
        }
        catch (JsonException ex)
        {
            logger.LogWarning(ex, "Failed to parse saved {Type} map", typeof(T).Name);
            return [];
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Repositories;

public sealed class SettingsRepository(
    IDbContextFactory<AppDbContext> contextFactory,
    DatabaseInitializer initializer) : ISettingsRepository
{
    public async Task<PlayerSettings> LoadAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        await initializer.InitializeAsync(ct);
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);
        var record = await db.PlayerSettings.AsNoTracking().FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.Id == PlayerSettingsRecord.SingletonId, ct);
        return record is null ? PlayerSettings.Default : new PlayerSettings(record.SoundEnabled, record.HapticsEnabled);
    }

    public async Task SaveAsync(PlayerSettings settings, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(settings);

        await initializer.InitializeAsync(ct);
        await using var db = await contextFactory.CreateDbContextAsync(ct);
        var record = await db.PlayerSettings.FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.Id == PlayerSettingsRecord.SingletonId, ct);
        if (record is null)
        {
            record = new PlayerSettingsRecord();
            db.PlayerSettings.Add(record);
        }

        record.SoundEnabled = settings.SoundEnabled;
        record.HapticsEnabled = settings.HapticsEnabled;
        await db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);
    }
}

SIZE: 3.45 KB

using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

public sealed record SentryDsn
{
    public required string Raw { get; init; }
    public required string PublicKey { get; init; }
    public required string SecretKey { get; init; }
    public required string ProjectId { get; init; }
    public required string Host { get; init; }
    public required string LogsEndpoint { get; init; }
    public required string TracesEndpoint { get; init; }
    public required string EnvelopeEndpoint { get; init; }
    public required string AuthHeaderValue { get; init; }

    public static bool TryParse(string? dsn, [NotNullWhen(true)] out SentryDsn? parsed, out string? error)
    {
        parsed = null;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dsn))
        {
            error = "DSN is empty.";
            return false;
        }

        try
        {
            parsed = Parse(dsn);
            error = null;
            return true;
        }
        catch (ArgumentException ex)
        {
            error = ex.Message;
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static SentryDsn Parse(string dsn)
    {
        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(dsn);

        var (uriCandidate, secretKey) = StripLegacySecretKey(dsn);

        if (!Uri.TryCreate(uriCandidate, UriKind.Absolute, out var uri))
        {
            throw new ArgumentException("DSN is not a valid absolute URI.", nameof(dsn));
        }

        if (uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttps && uri.Scheme != Uri.UriSchemeHttp)
        {
            throw new ArgumentException($"DSN scheme '{uri.Scheme}' is not supported. Sentry DSNs must be http or https.", nameof(dsn));
        }

        var publicKey = uri.UserInfo;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(publicKey))
        {
            throw new ArgumentException("DSN public key is missing.", nameof(dsn));
        }

        var projectId = uri.AbsolutePath.Split('/', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).FirstOrDefault()
            ?? throw new ArgumentException("DSN missing project ID metadata.", nameof(dsn));

        var host = uri.IsDefaultPort ? uri.Host : $"{uri.Host}:{uri.Port}";
        var apiBase = $"{uri.Scheme}://{host}/api/{projectId}";

        return new SentryDsn
        {
            Raw = dsn,
            PublicKey = publicKey,
            SecretKey = secretKey,
            ProjectId = projectId,
            Host = host,
            LogsEndpoint = $"{apiBase}/integration/otlp/v1/logs",
            TracesEndpoint = $"{apiBase}/integration/otlp/v1/traces",
            EnvelopeEndpoint = $"{apiBase}/envelope/",
            AuthHeaderValue = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(secretKey)
                ? $"sentry sentry_key={publicKey}"
                : $"sentry sentry_key={publicKey} sentry_secret={secretKey}",
        };
    }

    private static (string Rewritten, string SecretKey) StripLegacySecretKey(string dsn)
    {
        var schemeSeparator = dsn.IndexOf("://", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        if (schemeSeparator < 0) return (dsn, string.Empty);

        var userInfoStart = schemeSeparator + 3;
        var atSign = dsn.IndexOf('@', userInfoStart);
        if (atSign < 0) return (dsn, string.Empty);

        var userInfo = dsn.AsSpan(userInfoStart, atSign - userInfoStart);
        var colon = userInfo.IndexOf(':');
        if (colon < 0) return (dsn, string.Empty);

        return (string.Concat(dsn.AsSpan(0, userInfoStart), userInfo[..colon], dsn.AsSpan(atSign)), userInfo[(colon + 1)..].ToString());
    }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/MyAdventure.Infrastructure/Telemetry/SentryEnvelope.cs
SIZE: 5.86 KB
================================================================================

using System.Buffers;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text.Encodings.Web;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

internal static class SentryEnvelope
{
    public const string EventContentType = "application/json";

    private const string SdkName = "myadventure.otlp-envelope";
    private const byte Newline = (byte)'\n';

    private static readonly JsonWriterOptions WriterOptions = new()
    {
        Encoder = JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping,
    };

    public static byte[] BuildExceptionEvent(
        SentryEventPayload payload,
        SentryDsn dsn,
        string serviceName,
        string serviceVersion,
        string environment,
        DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(payload);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(dsn);

        var eventId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        var eventJson = Write(writer => WriteEvent(writer, payload, eventId, serviceName, serviceVersion, environment, now));

        var envelopeHeader = Write(writer =>
        {
            writer.WriteStartObject();
            writer.WriteString("event_id", eventId);
            writer.WriteString("dsn", dsn.Raw);
            writer.WriteString("sent_at", now.UtcDateTime.ToString("o", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            WriteSdk(writer, serviceVersion);
            writer.WriteEndObject();
        });

        var itemHeader = Write(writer =>
        {
            writer.WriteStartObject();
            writer.WriteString("type", "event");
            writer.WriteNumber("length", eventJson.Length);
            writer.WriteString("content_type", EventContentType);
            writer.WriteEndObject();
        });

        return [.. envelopeHeader, Newline, .. itemHeader, Newline, .. eventJson, Newline];
    }

    private static byte[] Write(Action<Utf8JsonWriter> write)
    {
        var buffer = new ArrayBufferWriter<byte>();
        using (var writer = new Utf8JsonWriter(buffer, WriterOptions))
        {
            write(writer);
        }
        return buffer.WrittenSpan.ToArray();
    }

    private static void WriteEvent(
        Utf8JsonWriter writer,
        SentryEventPayload payload,
        string eventId,
        string serviceName,
        string serviceVersion,
        string environment,
        DateTimeOffset now)
    {
        writer.WriteStartObject();
        writer.WriteString("event_id", eventId);
        writer.WriteNumber("timestamp", now.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds() / 1000.0);
        writer.WriteString("platform", "csharp");
        writer.WriteString("level", payload.Level);
        writer.WriteString("logger", payload.LoggerCategory);
        writer.WriteString("server_name", Environment.MachineName);
        writer.WriteString("environment", environment);
        writer.WriteString("release", $"{serviceName}@{serviceVersion}");

        writer.WriteStartObject("message");
        writer.WriteString("formatted", payload.Message);
        writer.WriteEndObject();

        writer.WriteStartObject("exception");
        writer.WriteStartArray("values");
        foreach (var exception in ExceptionChain(payload.Exception))
        {
            WriteException(writer, exception);
        }
        writer.WriteEndArray();
        writer.WriteEndObject();

        WriteSdk(writer, serviceVersion);

        if (payload.Tags is { Count: > 0 } tags)
        {
            writer.WriteStartObject("tags");
            foreach (var (key, value) in tags)
            {
                writer.WriteString(key, value);
            }
            writer.WriteEndObject();
        }

        writer.WriteEndObject();
    }

    private static IEnumerable<Exception> ExceptionChain(Exception outermost)
    {
        var chain = new Stack<Exception>();
        for (var current = outermost; current is not null; current = current.InnerException)
        {
            chain.Push(current);
        }
        return chain;
    }

    private static void WriteException(Utf8JsonWriter writer, Exception exception)
    {
        var type = exception.GetType();
        writer.WriteStartObject();
        writer.WriteString("type", type.FullName ?? type.Name);
        writer.WriteString("value", exception.Message);
        writer.WriteString("module", type.Namespace ?? string.Empty);

        writer.WriteStartObject("stacktrace");
        writer.WriteStartArray("frames");
        var frames = new StackTrace(exception, fNeedFileInfo: true).GetFrames();
        for (var i = frames.Length - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            WriteFrame(writer, frames[i]);
        }
        writer.WriteEndArray();
        writer.WriteEndObject();

        writer.WriteEndObject();
    }

    private static void WriteFrame(Utf8JsonWriter writer, StackFrame frame)
    {
        if (frame.GetMethod() is not { } method) return;

        var module = method.DeclaringType?.FullName ?? "<unknown>";
        writer.WriteStartObject();
        writer.WriteString("function", method.Name);
        writer.WriteString("module", module);
        writer.WriteBoolean("in_app", module.StartsWith("MyAdventure", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        if (frame.GetFileName() is { Length: > 0 } fileName)
        {
            writer.WriteString("filename", fileName);
            writer.WriteString("abs_path", fileName);
        }

        if (frame.GetFileLineNumber() is > 0 and var line)
        {
            writer.WriteNumber("lineno", line);
        }

        writer.WriteEndObject();
    }

    private static void WriteSdk(Utf8JsonWriter writer, string serviceVersion)
    {
        writer.WriteStartObject("sdk");
        writer.WriteString("name", SdkName);
        writer.WriteString("version", serviceVersion);
        writer.WriteEndObject();
    }
}

internal sealed record SentryEventPayload(
    Exception Exception,
    string Message,
    string Level,
    string LoggerCategory,
    IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string>? Tags = null);

using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

internal sealed class SentryEventLoggerProvider : ILoggerProvider
{
    private const string EnvelopeContentType = "application/x-sentry-envelope";

    private readonly SentryDsn _dsn;
    private readonly HttpClient _http;
    private readonly bool _ownsHttpClient;
    private readonly string _serviceName;
    private readonly string _serviceVersion;
    private readonly string _environment;
    private readonly TimeProvider _timeProvider;
    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, SentryEventLogger> _loggers = new();
    private readonly CancellationTokenSource _cts = new();

    public SentryEventLoggerProvider(SentryDsn dsn, string serviceName, string serviceVersion, string environment)
        : this(dsn, new HttpClient { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10) }, ownsHttpClient: true, serviceName, serviceVersion, environment, TimeProvider.System)
    {
    }

    internal SentryEventLoggerProvider(SentryDsn dsn, HttpClient http, string serviceName, string serviceVersion, string environment, TimeProvider timeProvider)
        : this(dsn, http, ownsHttpClient: false, serviceName, serviceVersion, environment, timeProvider)
    {
    }

    private SentryEventLoggerProvider(
        SentryDsn dsn,
        HttpClient http,
        bool ownsHttpClient,
        string serviceName,
        string serviceVersion,
        string environment,
        TimeProvider timeProvider)
    {
        _dsn = dsn ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(dsn));
        _http = http ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(http));
        _serviceName = serviceName ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(serviceName));
        _serviceVersion = serviceVersion ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(serviceVersion));
        _environment = environment ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(environment));
        _timeProvider = timeProvider ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(timeProvider));
        _ownsHttpClient = ownsHttpClient;
    }

    public ILogger CreateLogger(string categoryName) =>
        _loggers.GetOrAdd(categoryName, static (name, provider) => new SentryEventLogger(provider, name), this);

    public void Dispose()
    {
        _cts.Cancel();
        if (_ownsHttpClient) _http.Dispose();
        _cts.Dispose();
    }

    internal static bool ShouldPromoteToIssue(LogLevel level, Exception? exception) =>
        exception is not null || level == LogLevel.Critical;

    internal void Enqueue(SentryEventPayload payload)
    {
        byte[] bytes;
        try
        {
            bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(payload, _dsn, _serviceName, _serviceVersion, _environment, _timeProvider.GetUtcNow());
        }
        catch
        {
            return;
        }

        _ = SendAsync(bytes, _cts.Token);
    }

    private async Task SendAsync(byte[] bytes, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        try
        {
            using var request = new HttpRequestMessage(HttpMethod.Post, _dsn.EnvelopeEndpoint)
            {
                Content = new ByteArrayContent(bytes) { Headers = { ContentType = new MediaTypeHeaderValue(EnvelopeContentType) } },
            };
            request.Headers.TryAddWithoutValidation("X-Sentry-Auth", _dsn.AuthHeaderValue);
            using var response = await _http.SendAsync(request, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);
        }
        catch
        {
        }
    }
}

internal sealed class SentryEventLogger(SentryEventLoggerProvider provider, string category) : ILogger
{
    public IDisposable? BeginScope<TState>(TState state) where TState : notnull => null;

    public bool IsEnabled(LogLevel logLevel) => logLevel >= LogLevel.Warning;

    public void Log<TState>(LogLevel logLevel, EventId eventId, TState state, Exception? exception, Func<TState, Exception?, string> formatter)
    {
        if (!SentryEventLoggerProvider.ShouldPromoteToIssue(logLevel, exception)) return;

        provider.Enqueue(new SentryEventPayload(
            Exception: exception ?? new SentryCriticalLogException(formatter(state, null)),
            Message: formatter(state, exception),
            Level: ToSentryLevel(logLevel),
            LoggerCategory: category));
    }

    private static string ToSentryLevel(LogLevel level) => level switch
    {
        LogLevel.Critical => "fatal",
        LogLevel.Error => "error",
        LogLevel.Warning => "warning",
        LogLevel.Information => "info",
        LogLevel.Debug or LogLevel.Trace => "debug",
        _ => "error",
    };
}

internal sealed class SentryCriticalLogException(string message) : Exception(message);

using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

public static class TelemetryConfigurationLoader
{
    public const string SentryDsnEnvVar = "SENTRY_DSN";
    public const string VerboseLoggingEnvVar = "MYADVENTURE_VERBOSE";
    public const string SentryEnvironmentEnvVar = "MYADVENTURE_SENTRY_ENVIRONMENT";

    public static TelemetryOptions Load(string? baseDirectory = null)
    {
        var directory = baseDirectory ?? AppContext.BaseDirectory;
        var builder = new ConfigurationBuilder();
        if (Path.IsPathRooted(directory) && Directory.Exists(directory))
        {
            builder.SetBasePath(directory)
                .AddJsonFile("appsettings.json", optional: true, reloadOnChange: false)
                .AddJsonFile("appsettings.local.json", optional: true, reloadOnChange: false);
        }

        return LoadFromConfiguration(builder.AddEnvironmentVariables().Build());
    }

    public static TelemetryOptions LoadFromConfiguration(IConfiguration configuration) =>
        LoadFromConfiguration(configuration, TelemetryDefaults.DefaultDsn);

    internal static TelemetryOptions LoadFromConfiguration(IConfiguration configuration, string fallbackDsn)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(configuration);

        var options = new TelemetryOptions();
        configuration.GetSection(TelemetryOptions.SectionName).Bind(options);

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(options.Sentry.Dsn) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fallbackDsn))
        {
            options.Sentry.Dsn = fallbackDsn;
        }

        ApplyEnvironmentOverrides(options);
        return options;
    }

    private static void ApplyEnvironmentOverrides(TelemetryOptions options)
    {
        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(SentryDsnEnvVar) is { } dsn && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dsn))
        {
            options.Sentry.Dsn = dsn;
        }

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(VerboseLoggingEnvVar) is { } verbose && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(verbose))
        {
            options.VerboseLogging = ParseBool(verbose);
        }

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(SentryEnvironmentEnvVar) is { } environment && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(environment))
        {
            options.Sentry.Environment = environment;
        }
    }

    private static bool ParseBool(string raw) => raw.Trim().ToLowerInvariant() is "true" or "1" or "yes" or "on";
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using OpenTelemetry.Metrics;
using OpenTelemetry.Trace;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

public enum TelemetryFlushMode
{
    Soft,
    Final,
}

public static class TelemetryLifecycle
{
    public static void Start(IServiceProvider services)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(services);

        _ = services.GetService<TracerProvider>();
        _ = services.GetService<MeterProvider>();

        var status = services.GetRequiredService<TelemetryStatus>();
        var logger = services.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger("MyAdventure.Telemetry");

        if (status.SentryEnabled)
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Telemetry: Sentry enabled (OTLP logs/traces + envelope issues), env={Environment}, verbose={Verbose}", status.Environment, status.VerboseLogging);
        }
        else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(status.SentryParseError))
        {
            logger.LogWarning("Telemetry: Sentry DSN was provided but could not be parsed ({Error}); console-only.", status.SentryParseError);
        }
        else
        {
            logger.LogInformation("Telemetry: Sentry disabled (no DSN), verbose={Verbose}", status.VerboseLogging);
        }
    }

    public static Task FlushAsync(IServiceProvider services, TelemetryFlushMode mode = TelemetryFlushMode.Soft, int timeoutMilliseconds = 2000)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(services);

        return Task.Run(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                services.GetService<TracerProvider>()?.ForceFlush(timeoutMilliseconds);
                if (mode == TelemetryFlushMode.Final && services is IDisposable disposable) disposable.Dispose();
            }
            catch
            {
            }
        });
    }
}

using System.Reflection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using OpenTelemetry.Exporter;
using OpenTelemetry.Logs;
using OpenTelemetry.Metrics;
using OpenTelemetry.Resources;
using OpenTelemetry.Trace;

namespace MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;

internal static class TelemetryRegistration
{
    private const string SourcePattern = "MyAdventure.*";

    public static IServiceCollection AddTelemetry(this IServiceCollection services, TelemetryOptions telemetry)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(telemetry);

        var serviceVersion = ServiceVersion();
        var resource = ResourceBuilder.CreateDefault()
            .AddService(TelemetryDefaults.ServiceName, serviceVersion: serviceVersion, serviceInstanceId: Environment.MachineName)
            .AddAttributes(new Dictionary<string, object> { ["deployment.environment"] = telemetry.Sentry.Environment });

        string? parseError = null;
        var sentry = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(telemetry.Sentry.Dsn) && SentryDsn.TryParse(telemetry.Sentry.Dsn, out var parsed, out parseError)
            ? parsed
            : null;

        services.AddSingleton(telemetry);
        services.AddSingleton(new TelemetryStatus(sentry is not null, parseError, telemetry.VerboseLogging, telemetry.Sentry.Environment));

        services.AddLogging(logging =>
        {
            logging.AddFilter("Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore", telemetry.VerboseLogging ? LogLevel.Information : LogLevel.Warning);
            logging.SetMinimumLevel(telemetry.VerboseLogging ? LogLevel.Debug : LogLevel.Information);
            logging.AddOpenTelemetry(otel =>
            {
                otel.SetResourceBuilder(resource);
                otel.IncludeFormattedMessage = true;
                otel.IncludeScopes = true;
                otel.ParseStateValues = true;
                otel.AddConsoleExporter();
                if (sentry is not null) otel.AddOtlpExporter(o => ConfigureOtlp(o, sentry.LogsEndpoint, sentry));
            });

            if (sentry is not null)
            {
                logging.AddProvider(new SentryEventLoggerProvider(sentry, TelemetryDefaults.ServiceName, serviceVersion, telemetry.Sentry.Environment));
            }
        });

        services.AddOpenTelemetry()
            .WithTracing(tracing =>
            {
                tracing.SetResourceBuilder(resource);
                tracing.AddSource(SourcePattern);
                tracing.SetSampler(new TraceIdRatioBasedSampler(Math.Clamp(telemetry.Sentry.TracesSampleRate, 0.0, 1.0)));
                tracing.AddConsoleExporter();
                if (sentry is not null) tracing.AddOtlpExporter(o => ConfigureOtlp(o, sentry.TracesEndpoint, sentry));
            })
            .WithMetrics(metrics =>
            {
                metrics.SetResourceBuilder(resource);
                metrics.AddMeter(SourcePattern);
                metrics.AddRuntimeInstrumentation();
                metrics.AddConsoleExporter();
            });

        return services;
    }

    private static void ConfigureOtlp(OtlpExporterOptions options, string endpoint, SentryDsn sentry)
    {
        options.Endpoint = new Uri(endpoint);
        options.Protocol = OtlpExportProtocol.HttpProtobuf;
        options.Headers = $"x-sentry-auth={sentry.AuthHeaderValue}";
    }

    private static string ServiceVersion()
    {
        var assembly = typeof(TelemetryRegistration).Assembly;
        return assembly.GetCustomAttribute<AssemblyInformationalVersionAttribute>()?.InformationalVersion is { Length: > 0 } version
            ? version
            : assembly.GetName().Version?.ToString() ?? "0.0.0";
    }
}

internal sealed record TelemetryStatus(bool SentryEnabled, string? SentryParseError, bool VerboseLogging, string Environment);

using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes;
using Avalonia.Styling;
using Avalonia.Themes.Fluent2;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Views;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared;

public class App : Application
{
    private readonly LifecycleRouter _lifecycle = new();
    private ServiceProvider? _services;

    public override void Initialize()
    {
        RequestedThemeVariant = ThemeVariant.Dark;
        Styles.Add(new Fluent2Theme());
    }

    public override void OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted()
    {
        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        ConfigurePlatformServices(services);
        services.AddInfrastructure(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.Load()).AddGame();
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();
        _services = provider;

        TelemetryLifecycle.Start(provider);
        ObserveUnhandledExceptions(provider.GetRequiredService<ILoggerFactory>().CreateLogger<App>());
        ObserveLifecycle(provider);

        switch (ApplicationLifetime)
        {
            case IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime desktop:
                desktop.MainWindow = new MainWindow(CreateMainView(provider));
                desktop.Exit += (_, _) => Shutdown(provider);
                break;
            case IActivityApplicationLifetime activity:
                activity.MainViewFactory = () => CreateMainView(provider);
                break;
            case ISingleViewApplicationLifetime singleView:
                singleView.MainView = CreateMainView(provider);
                break;
        }

        base.OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted();
    }

    protected virtual void ConfigurePlatformServices(IServiceCollection services)
    {
    }

    private Control CreateMainView(IServiceProvider provider)
    {
        var viewModel = provider.GetRequiredService<GameViewModel>();
        _lifecycle.Attach(viewModel);
        return new MainView { DataContext = viewModel };
    }

    private void ObserveLifecycle(IServiceProvider provider)
    {
        _lifecycle.Backgrounded += (_, _) => _ = TelemetryLifecycle.FlushAsync(provider, TelemetryFlushMode.Soft);

        if (this.TryGetFeature<IActivatableLifetime>() is not { } activatable) return;
        activatable.Activated += (_, e) => _lifecycle.OnActivated(e.Kind);
        activatable.Deactivated += (_, e) => _lifecycle.OnDeactivated(e.Kind);
    }

    private void Shutdown(IServiceProvider provider)
    {
        if (_services is null) return;
        _services = null;

        var viewModel = provider.GetRequiredService<GameViewModel>();
        Task.Run(() => viewModel.SaveAsync()).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
        TelemetryLifecycle.FlushAsync(provider, TelemetryFlushMode.Final).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
    }

    private static void ObserveUnhandledExceptions(ILogger logger)
    {
        AppDomain.CurrentDomain.UnhandledException += (_, e) =>
            logger.LogCritical(e.ExceptionObject as Exception, "Unhandled exception (terminating: {Terminating})", e.IsTerminating);
        TaskScheduler.UnobservedTaskException += (_, e) =>
        {
            logger.LogError(e.Exception, "Unobserved task exception");
            e.SetObserved();
        };
    }
}

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Converters;

using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

public sealed class FeedbackService(ISoundPlayer sound, IHapticPlayer haptics, ILogger<FeedbackService> logger) : IFeedbackService
{
    public bool SoundEnabled { get; set; } = true;

    public bool HapticsEnabled { get; set; } = true;

    public bool HapticsSupported => haptics.IsSupported;

    public void Play(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        try
        {
            if (SoundEnabled) sound.Play(cue);
            if (HapticsEnabled && haptics.IsSupported) haptics.Play(cue);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            logger.LogDebug(ex, "Feedback cue {Cue} failed", cue);
        }
    }
}

using System.Buffers.Binary;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;

public static class ToneSynthesizer
{
    public const int SampleRate = 44_100;
    public const int HeaderSize = 44;

    private const short Channels = 1;
    private const short BitsPerSample = 16;
    private const short BlockAlign = Channels * BitsPerSample / 8;
    private const double Volume = 0.3;
    private const double AttackSeconds = 0.002;

    private readonly record struct Note(double StartHz, double EndHz, double Seconds, bool Square = false);

    public static byte[] Render(FeedbackCue cue) => ToWav(Synthesize(Score(cue)));

    private static Note[] Score(FeedbackCue cue) => cue switch
    {
        FeedbackCue.Tap => [new(1_400, 1_100, 0.025)],
        FeedbackCue.Purchase => [new(988, 988, 0.05), new(1_319, 1_319, 0.09)],
        FeedbackCue.Manager => [new(659, 659, 0.05), new(880, 880, 0.05), new(1_109, 1_109, 0.1)],
        FeedbackCue.Milestone => [new(523, 523, 0.06), new(659, 659, 0.06), new(784, 784, 0.06), new(1_047, 1_047, 0.16)],
        FeedbackCue.Prestige => [new(262, 1_047, 0.35), new(1_047, 1_568, 0.2)],
        FeedbackCue.Denied => [new(196, 175, 0.07, Square: true), new(0, 0, 0.03), new(175, 156, 0.09, Square: true)],
        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(cue), cue, null),
    };

    private static short[] Synthesize(Note[] notes)
    {
        var samples = new short[notes.Sum(n => SampleCount(n.Seconds))];
        var offset = 0;
        var phase = 0.0;

        foreach (var note in notes)
        {
            var count = SampleCount(note.Seconds);
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var t = (double)i / SampleRate;
                var progress = (double)i / count;
                var frequency = note.StartHz + (note.EndHz - note.StartHz) * progress;
                phase += 2 * Math.PI * frequency / SampleRate;

                var wave = Math.Sin(phase);
                if (note.Square) wave = Math.Sign(wave) * 0.5;

                var envelope = Math.Min(1.0, t / AttackSeconds) * Math.Pow(1.0 - progress, 2);
                samples[offset + i] = frequency <= 0 ? (short)0 : (short)(wave * envelope * Volume * short.MaxValue);
            }
            offset += count;
        }

        return samples;
    }

    private static int SampleCount(double seconds) => (int)(seconds * SampleRate);

    private static byte[] ToWav(short[] samples)
    {
        var dataSize = samples.Length * sizeof(short);
        var wav = new byte[HeaderSize + dataSize];
        var span = wav.AsSpan();

        "RIFF"u8.CopyTo(span);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32LittleEndian(span[4..], HeaderSize - 8 + dataSize);
        "WAVE"u8.CopyTo(span[8..]);
        "fmt "u8.CopyTo(span[12..]);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32LittleEndian(span[16..], 16);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt16LittleEndian(span[20..], 1);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt16LittleEndian(span[22..], Channels);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32LittleEndian(span[24..], SampleRate);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32LittleEndian(span[28..], SampleRate * BlockAlign);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt16LittleEndian(span[32..], BlockAlign);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt16LittleEndian(span[34..], BitsPerSample);
        "data"u8.CopyTo(span[36..]);
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32LittleEndian(span[40..], dataSize);

        var data = span[HeaderSize..];
        for (var i = 0; i < samples.Length; i++)
        {
            BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt16LittleEndian(data[(i * sizeof(short))..], samples[i]);
        }

        return wav;
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.Extensions;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared;

public static class GameServiceCollectionExtensions
{
    extension(IServiceCollection services)
    {
        public IServiceCollection AddGame()
        {
            services.TryAddSingleton(TimeProvider.System);
            services.TryAddSingleton<ISoundPlayer, SilentSoundPlayer>();
            services.TryAddSingleton<IHapticPlayer, NoHaptics>();
            services.AddSingleton<IFeedbackService, FeedbackService>();
            services.AddSingleton<ToastService>();
            services.AddSingleton<GameEngine>();
            services.AddSingleton<GameViewModel>();
            return services;
        }
    }

{

    {

FILE: src/MyAdventure.Shared/Services/LifecycleRouter.cs

using Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Services;

public interface ILifecycleAware
{
    void OnSuspended();

    void OnResumed();
}

public sealed class LifecycleRouter
{
    private ILifecycleAware? _target;

    public event EventHandler? Backgrounded;

    public void Attach(ILifecycleAware target) => _target = target ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(target));

    public void Detach(ILifecycleAware target)
    {
        if (ReferenceEquals(_target, target)) _target = null;
    }

    public void OnActivated(ActivationKind kind)
    {
        if (kind == ActivationKind.Background) _target?.OnResumed();
    }

    public void OnDeactivated(ActivationKind kind)
    {
        if (kind != ActivationKind.Background) return;
        _target?.OnSuspended();
        Backgrounded?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
}

FILE: src/MyAdventure.Shared/Services/ToastService.cs
SIZE: .81 KB

using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Services;

public sealed record ToastItem(string Message, DateTimeOffset ExpiresAt);

public sealed class ToastService(TimeProvider time)
{
    public static TimeSpan DefaultLifetime { get; } = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(3);

    public ToastService() : this(TimeProvider.System)
    {
    }

    public ObservableCollection<ToastItem> ActiveToasts { get; } = [];

    public void Show(string message, TimeSpan? lifetime = null) =>
        ActiveToasts.Add(new ToastItem(message, time.GetUtcNow() + (lifetime ?? DefaultLifetime)));

    public void CleanupExpired()
    {
        var now = time.GetUtcNow();
        for (var i = ActiveToasts.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            if (ActiveToasts[i].ExpiresAt <= now) ActiveToasts.RemoveAt(i);
        }
    }
}

using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class BusinessViewModel(
    Business model,
    GameEngine engine,
    ToastService toasts,
    IFeedbackService feedback) : ObservableObject
{
    public Business Model => model;
    public string Id => model.Id;
    public string Name => model.Name;
    public string Icon => model.Icon;
    public string Color => model.Color;

    public long Owned { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool IsOwned { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public double ProgressPercent { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool IsRunning { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool HasManager { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string CostText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public string RevenueText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public string ManagerCostText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public bool CanAfford { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool CanAffordManager { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string CycleTimeText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public string RevenuePerSecondText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public long AffordableCount { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string AffordableCountText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public double MilestoneMultiplier { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = 1.0;
    public string MilestoneMultiplierText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "×1";
    public string NextMilestoneText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public long UnitsToNextMilestone { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool HasNextMilestone { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string NextMilestoneRewardText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public double SpeedMultiplier { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = 1.0;
    public string SpeedMultiplierText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "×1 Speed";
    public bool HasSpeedBonus { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool HasNextSpeedMilestone { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string NextSpeedMilestoneText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public bool CanBulkBuy { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string BulkBuyText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";

    [RelayCommand]
    private void ClickBusiness()
    {
        if (model.Owned <= 0)
        {
            if (TryPurchase(() => engine.BuyBusiness(model.Id))) return;
            Deny($"Need ${NumberFormatter.Format(model.NextCost)} to buy your first {model.Name}");
            return;
        }

        if (engine.StartBusiness(model.Id))
        {
            feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
            return;
        }

        if (model.IsRunning)
        {
            var remaining = model.CycleTimeSeconds * (1.0 - model.ProgressPercent / 100.0);
            toasts.Show($"{model.Name} is running — {remaining:F1}s left");
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void BuyBusiness()
    {
        if (TryPurchase(() => engine.BuyBusiness(model.Id))) return;
        Deny($"Need ${NumberFormatter.Format(model.NextCost - engine.Cash)} more for next {model.Name}");
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void BuyManager()
    {
        if (model.HasManager)
        {
            toasts.Show($"{model.Name} already has a manager");
            return;
        }

        if (engine.BuyManager(model.Id))
        {
            feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Manager);
            return;
        }

        Deny($"Need ${NumberFormatter.Format(GameBalance.ManagerCost(model) - engine.Cash)} more for {model.Name} manager");
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void BulkBuy()
    {
        if (Milestone.NextMilestone(model.Owned) is not { } next)
        {
            if (model.AffordableCount(engine.Cash) <= 0)
            {
                Deny($"Can't afford any more {model.Name} right now");
                return;
            }

            var bought = 0L;
            if (TryPurchase(() => (bought = engine.BuyMax(model.Id)) > 0))
            {
                toasts.Show($"Bought {bought} more {model.Name} (now {model.Owned})");
            }
            return;
        }

        var needed = next.Threshold - model.Owned;
        if (needed <= 0) return;

        var purchased = 0L;
        if (!TryPurchase(() => (purchased = engine.BuyMultiple(model.Id, needed)) > 0))
        {
            Deny($"Can't afford any more {model.Name} right now");
        }
        else if (purchased < needed)
        {
            toasts.Show($"Bought {purchased} {model.Name} — need {needed - purchased} more for milestone");
        }
        else
        {
            toasts.Show($"Milestone reached! {model.Name} now at {model.Owned} ({next.Label})");
        }
    }

    public void Refresh(BigDouble cash, BigDouble angelBonus) => Refresh(cash, angelBonus, BigDouble.One);

    public void Refresh(BigDouble cash, BigDouble angelBonus, BigDouble crossBusinessSpeed)
    {
        Owned = model.Owned;
        IsOwned = model.Owned > 0;
        ProgressPercent = model.ProgressPercent;
        IsRunning = model.IsRunning;
        HasManager = model.HasManager;
        CostText = NumberFormatter.Format(model.NextCost);

        var earningsBonus = angelBonus * crossBusinessSpeed;
        RevenueText = IsOwned ? NumberFormatter.Format(model.Revenue * earningsBonus) : "—";

        var managerCost = GameBalance.ManagerCost(model);
        ManagerCostText = NumberFormatter.Format(managerCost);
        CanAfford = cash >= model.NextCost;
        CanAffordManager = !model.HasManager && cash >= managerCost;

        CycleTimeText = FormatTime(model.CycleTimeSeconds);
        RevenuePerSecondText = IsOwned ? $"${NumberFormatter.Format(model.RevenuePerSecond * earningsBonus)}/s" : "—";

        AffordableCount = model.AffordableCount(cash);
        AffordableCountText = AffordableCount > 0 ? $"Can buy: {AffordableCount}" : "Can't afford";

        MilestoneMultiplier = model.MilestoneMultiplier;
        MilestoneMultiplierText = $"×{MilestoneMultiplier:G4}";

        SpeedMultiplier = model.SpeedMultiplier;
        SpeedMultiplierText = $"×{SpeedMultiplier:G4} Speed";
        HasSpeedBonus = SpeedMultiplier > 1.0;

        var nextSpeed = SpeedMilestone.NextSpeedMilestone(model.Owned);
        HasNextSpeedMilestone = nextSpeed is not null;
        NextSpeedMilestoneText = nextSpeed is null ? "" : $"{nextSpeed.Threshold - model.Owned} more → {nextSpeed.Threshold} ({nextSpeed.Label})";

        if (Milestone.NextMilestone(model.Owned) is { } next)
        {
            HasNextMilestone = true;
            UnitsToNextMilestone = next.Threshold - model.Owned;
            NextMilestoneText = $"{UnitsToNextMilestone} more → {next.Threshold}";
            NextMilestoneRewardText = next.Label;
            CanBulkBuy = CanAfford && UnitsToNextMilestone > 0;
            BulkBuyText = $"BUY {UnitsToNextMilestone}→{next.Threshold}";
        }
        else
        {
            HasNextMilestone = false;
            UnitsToNextMilestone = 0;
            NextMilestoneText = "All milestones reached!";
            NextMilestoneRewardText = "";
            CanBulkBuy = AffordableCount > 0;
            BulkBuyText = AffordableCount > 0 ? $"BUY MAX ({AffordableCount})" : "BUY MAX";
        }
    }

    private bool TryPurchase(Func<bool> purchase)
    {
        var milestoneBefore = model.MilestoneMultiplier;
        var speedBefore = model.SpeedMultiplier;
        if (!purchase()) return false;

        var crossedMilestone = model.MilestoneMultiplier > milestoneBefore || model.SpeedMultiplier > speedBefore;
        feedback.Play(crossedMilestone ? FeedbackCue.Milestone : FeedbackCue.Purchase);
        return true;
    }

    private void Deny(string message)
    {
        feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);
        toasts.Show(message);
    }

    private static string FormatTime(double seconds) => seconds switch
    {
        < 1 => $"{seconds * 1000:F0}ms",
        < 60 => $"{seconds:F1}s",
        < 3600 => $"{seconds / 60:F1}m",
        _ => $"{seconds / 3600:F1}h",
    };
}

FILE: src/MyAdventure.Shared/ViewModels/GameViewModel.cs

using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;

public sealed partial class GameViewModel : ObservableObject, ILifecycleAware
{
    private const double MaxTickSeconds = 1.0;
    private const int TicksPerAutosave = 300;

    private readonly GameEngine _engine;
    private readonly ISettingsRepository _settings;
    private readonly IFeedbackService _feedback;
    private readonly ILogger<GameViewModel> _logger;
    private readonly TimeProvider _time;
    private Task? _initialization;
    private DateTimeOffset _lastTick;
    private DateTimeOffset? _suspendedAt;
    private int _ticksSinceSave;

    public GameViewModel(
        GameEngine engine,
        ToastService toasts,
        ISettingsRepository settings,
        IFeedbackService feedback,
        ILogger<GameViewModel> logger,
        TimeProvider time)
    {
        _engine = engine;
        _settings = settings;
        _feedback = feedback;
        _logger = logger;
        _time = time;
        Toasts = toasts;
        _lastTick = time.GetUtcNow();
    }

    public ObservableCollection<BusinessViewModel> Businesses { get; } = [];

    public ToastService Toasts { get; }

    public ITextClipboard? Clipboard { get; set; }

    public string CashText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "$0.00";
    public string AngelText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "0";
    public string AngelBonusText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "×1";
    public int PrestigeCount { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool CanPrestige { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string NextAngelText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "0";
    public string PrestigeExplanation { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public string CrossSpeedText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "×1 Speed";
    public bool HasCrossSpeedBonus { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string NextCrossThresholdText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";
    public string MinOwnedAcrossText { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "0";
    public bool IsTransferOpen { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public bool IsExportMode { get; private set => SetProperty(ref field, value); }
    public string TransferText { get; set => SetProperty(ref field, value); } = "";

    public bool SoundEnabled
    {
        get => _feedback.SoundEnabled;
        private set
        {
            if (_feedback.SoundEnabled == value) return;
            _feedback.SoundEnabled = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    public bool HapticsEnabled
    {
        get => _feedback.HapticsEnabled;
        private set
        {
            if (_feedback.HapticsEnabled == value) return;
            _feedback.HapticsEnabled = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();
        }
    }

    public bool HapticsSupported => _feedback.HapticsSupported;

    public Task InitializeAsync() => _initialization ??= LoadAsync();

    public void OnTick()
    {
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        var delta = Math.Min((now - _lastTick).TotalSeconds, MaxTickSeconds);
        _lastTick = now;

        _engine.Tick(delta);
        RefreshAll();
        Toasts.CleanupExpired();

        if (++_ticksSinceSave >= TicksPerAutosave)
        {
            _ticksSinceSave = 0;
            _ = SaveAsync();
        }
    }

    public void OnSuspended()
    {
        _suspendedAt = _time.GetUtcNow();
        _logger.LogInformation("App suspended at {SuspendedAt:o}", _suspendedAt);
        _ = SaveAsync();
    }

    public void OnResumed()
    {
        var now = _time.GetUtcNow();
        _lastTick = now;

        if (_suspendedAt is not { } suspended) return;
        _suspendedAt = null;

        var elapsed = now - suspended;
        var earned = _engine.ApplyOfflineEarnings(elapsed);
        if (earned.Sign > 0)
        {
            _logger.LogInformation("Applied resume earnings: {Earned} for {Seconds:F0}s suspended", earned.ToCanonicalString(), elapsed.TotalSeconds);
            Toasts.Show($"While you were away, you earned ${NumberFormatter.Format(earned)}!");
        }

        RefreshAll();
    }

    public async Task SaveAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            await _engine.SaveAsync();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to save game");
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void Export()
    {
        TransferText = _engine.ExportToString();
        IsExportMode = true;
        IsTransferOpen = true;
        _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
        _logger.LogInformation("Exported game state ({Length} chars)", TransferText.Length);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task CopyExportAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            if (Clipboard is { } clipboard && await clipboard.TrySetTextAsync(TransferText))
            {
                _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
                Toasts.Show("Copied to clipboard!");
            }
            else
            {
                Toasts.Show("Clipboard not available on this platform. Select all and copy manually.");
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Failed to copy to clipboard");
            Toasts.Show("Could not copy — select the text and copy manually.");
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void StartImport()
    {
        TransferText = "";
        IsExportMode = false;
        IsTransferOpen = true;
        _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task ConfirmImportAsync()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(TransferText))
        {
            Deny("Paste an export string first!");
            return;
        }

        if (!_engine.ImportFromString(TransferText))
        {
            Deny("Invalid import string. Check and try again.");
            return;
        }

        RebuildBusinesses();
        await SaveAsync();
        CloseTransfer();
        _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Purchase);
        Toasts.Show("Progress imported successfully!");
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CloseTransfer()
    {
        IsTransferOpen = false;
        TransferText = "";
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task PrestigeAsync()
    {
        if (!CanPrestige)
        {
            Deny("Prestige resets all businesses and cash, but you gain Angel Investors " +
                 "that permanently boost all revenue by +2% each. " +
                 "You need to earn more to unlock prestige (earn enough for at least 1 angel).");
            return;
        }

        var (angels, success) = _engine.Prestige();
        if (!success) return;

        RebuildBusinesses();
        await SaveAsync();
        _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Prestige);
        Toasts.Show($"Prestige! Gained {NumberFormatter.Format(angels)} angels. All revenue boosted!");
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private Task ToggleSoundAsync()
    {
        SoundEnabled = !SoundEnabled;
        if (SoundEnabled) _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
        return PersistSettingsAsync();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private Task ToggleHapticsAsync()
    {
        HapticsEnabled = !HapticsEnabled;
        if (HapticsEnabled) _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
        return PersistSettingsAsync();
    }

    private async Task LoadAsync()
    {
        var settings = await _settings.LoadAsync();
        SoundEnabled = settings.SoundEnabled;
        HapticsEnabled = settings.HapticsEnabled;

        await _engine.LoadAsync();
        _lastTick = _time.GetUtcNow();
        RebuildBusinesses();
        _logger.LogInformation("Game initialized with {Count} businesses", Businesses.Count);
    }

    private async Task PersistSettingsAsync()
    {
        try
        {
            await _settings.SaveAsync(new PlayerSettings(SoundEnabled, HapticsEnabled));
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to save settings");
        }
    }

    private void RebuildBusinesses()
    {
        Businesses.Clear();
        foreach (var business in _engine.Businesses)
        {
            Businesses.Add(new BusinessViewModel(business, _engine, Toasts, _feedback));
        }
        RefreshAll();
    }

    private void Deny(string message)
    {
        _feedback.Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);
        Toasts.Show(message);
    }

    private void RefreshAll()
    {
        CashText = $"${NumberFormatter.Format(_engine.Cash)}";
        AngelText = NumberFormatter.Format(_engine.AngelInvestors);
        AngelBonusText = $"×{NumberFormatter.Format(_engine.AngelBonus)}";
        PrestigeCount = _engine.PrestigeCount;

        var potentialAngels = _engine.PotentialAngels;
        CanPrestige = potentialAngels >= BigDouble.One;
        NextAngelText = potentialAngels.Sign > 0 ? NumberFormatter.Format(potentialAngels) : "0";
        PrestigeExplanation = CanPrestige
            ? $"Reset all businesses. Gain {NextAngelText} angels: income ×{NumberFormatter.Format(_engine.PrestigeIncomeMultiplier)}."
            : "Keep earning! Need enough lifetime earnings to gain at least 1 angel.";

        var angelBonus = _engine.AngelBonus;
        var crossBonus = _engine.CrossBusinessSpeedMultiplier;
        var cash = _engine.Cash;
        foreach (var business in Businesses)
        {
            business.Refresh(cash, angelBonus, crossBonus);
        }

        var minOwned = _engine.MinOwnedAcrossBusinesses;
        MinOwnedAcrossText = minOwned.ToString();
        HasCrossSpeedBonus = crossBonus > BigDouble.One;
        CrossSpeedText = HasCrossSpeedBonus ? $"×{NumberFormatter.Format(crossBonus)} Speed" : "×1 Speed";
        NextCrossThresholdText = CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.NextThreshold(minOwned) is { } nextThreshold
            ? $"{CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.UnitsToNext(minOwned)} more of every business → {nextThreshold}"
            : "All cross-business bonuses earned";
    }
}

FILE: src/MyAdventure.Shared/Views/MainView.axaml

<UserControl xmlns="https://github.com/avaloniaui"
             xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml"
             xmlns:vm="using:MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels"
             xmlns:conv="using:MyAdventure.Shared.Converters"
             xmlns:svc="using:MyAdventure.Shared.Services"
             x:Class="MyAdventure.Shared.Views.MainView"
             x:DataType="vm:GameViewModel"
             Background="#1A1A2E"
             TopLevel.AutoSafeAreaPadding="False">

    <UserControl.Styles>
        <Style Selector="Button">
            <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="White" />
            <Setter Property="FontWeight" Value="Bold" />
            <Setter Property="HorizontalContentAlignment" Value="Center" />
            <Setter Property="RenderTransform" Value="scale(1)" />

using Avalonia;
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Platform;
using Avalonia.Threading;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;

namespace MyAdventure.Shared.Views;

public partial class MainView : UserControl
{
    private static readonly TimeSpan FrameInterval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(16);

    private DispatcherTimer? _loop;
    private IInsetsManager? _insets;

    public MainView()
    {
        InitializeComponent();
    }

    protected override async void OnAttachedToVisualTree(VisualTreeAttachmentEventArgs e)
    {
        base.OnAttachedToVisualTree(e);

        _insets = TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this)?.InsetsManager;
        if (_insets is not null)
        {
            Padding = _insets.SafeAreaPadding;
            _insets.SafeAreaChanged += OnSafeAreaChanged;
        }

        if (DataContext is not GameViewModel vm) return;

        vm.Clipboard = new TopLevelClipboard(this);
        await vm.InitializeAsync();

        if (_loop is not null || TopLevel.GetTopLevel(this) is null) return;
        _loop = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = FrameInterval };
        _loop.Tick += (_, _) => vm.OnTick();
        _loop.Start();
    }

    protected override void OnDetachedFromVisualTree(VisualTreeAttachmentEventArgs e)
    {
        _loop?.Stop();
        _loop = null;

        if (_insets is not null)
        {
            _insets.SafeAreaChanged -= OnSafeAreaChanged;
            _insets = null;
        }

        base.OnDetachedFromVisualTree(e);
    }

    private void OnSafeAreaChanged(object? sender, SafeAreaChangedArgs e) => Padding = e.SafeAreaPadding;
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Core.Saves;

namespace MyAdventure.Testing;

public sealed class ManualTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset start) : TimeProvider
{
    private DateTimeOffset _now = start;

    public ManualTimeProvider() : this(new DateTimeOffset(2025, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero))
    {
    }

    public override DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => _now;

    public void Advance(TimeSpan by) => _now += by;
}

public sealed class InMemoryGameStateRepository(GameSnapshot? initial = null) : IGameStateRepository
{
    public GameSnapshot? Current { get; private set; } = initial;

    public List<GameSnapshot> Saved { get; } = [];

    public Task<GameSnapshot?> LoadAsync(CancellationToken ct = default) => Task.FromResult(Current);

    public Task SaveAsync(GameSnapshot snapshot, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        Current = snapshot;
        Saved.Add(snapshot);
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }

    public Task DeleteAllAsync(CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        Current = null;
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

public sealed class InMemorySettingsRepository(PlayerSettings? initial = null) : ISettingsRepository
{
    public PlayerSettings Current { get; private set; } = initial ?? PlayerSettings.Default;

    public int SaveCount { get; private set; }

    public Task<PlayerSettings> LoadAsync(CancellationToken ct = default) => Task.FromResult(Current);

    public Task SaveAsync(PlayerSettings settings, CancellationToken ct = default)
    {
        Current = settings;
        SaveCount++;
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Saves;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Tests.Balance;

public sealed record PrestigeEvent(int Number, double AtSeconds, double RunSeconds, double AngelsLog10, double LifetimeLog10);

public sealed record BalanceReport(
    double? FirstManagerSeconds,
    IReadOnlyList<PrestigeEvent> Prestiges,
    IReadOnlyList<(double AtSeconds, double IncomeLog10)> IncomeSamples,
    double EndSeconds,
    int Steps);

public sealed class BalanceBot
{
    private const double ClickIntervalSeconds = 1.0;
    private const double MinimumJumpSeconds = 1.01;
    private readonly GameEngine _engine;
    private readonly double _prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier;
    private double _now;
    private double _runStart;
    private double? _firstManager;
    private int _steps;
    private readonly List<PrestigeEvent> _prestiges = [];
    private readonly List<(double, double)> _samples = [];

    public BalanceBot(double prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier = 2.0, double seedLifetimeLog10 = double.NaN)
    {
        _prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier = prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier;
        _engine = new GameEngine(new InMemoryGameStateRepository(), NullLogger<GameEngine>.Instance);
        _engine.LoadAsync().GetAwaiter().GetResult();
        if (double.IsFinite(seedLifetimeLog10)) Seed(seedLifetimeLog10);
    }

    private void Seed(double lifetimeLog10)
    {
        var lifetime = new BigDouble(10.0).Pow(lifetimeLog10);
        var angels = GameEngine.CalculateAngels(lifetime);
        _engine.Restore(new GameSnapshot
        {
            Cash = new BigDouble(GameBalance.StartingCash),
            LifetimeEarnings = lifetime,
            AngelInvestors = angels,
            PrestigeCount = 1,
        });
    }

    public GameEngine Engine => _engine;

    public BalanceReport Play(double horizonSeconds, int maxPrestiges = int.MaxValue, int maxSteps = 200_000, IReadOnlyList<double>? sampleAt = null)
    {
        var samples = new Queue<double>(sampleAt ?? []);
        while (_now < horizonSeconds && _prestiges.Count < maxPrestiges && _steps < maxSteps)
        {
            _steps++;
            while (samples.Count > 0 && samples.Peek() <= _now)
            {
                samples.Dequeue();
                _samples.Add((_now, Log10(_engine.IncomePerSecond)));
            }

            if (_prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier > 0 && _engine.PotentialAngels >= BigDouble.One &&
                _engine.PrestigeIncomeMultiplier >= new BigDouble(_prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier))
            {
                var (_, ok) = _engine.Prestige();
                if (ok)
                {
                    _prestiges.Add(new PrestigeEvent(_engine.PrestigeCount, _now, _now - _runStart,
                        Log10(_engine.AngelInvestors), Log10(_engine.LifetimeEarnings)));
                    _runStart = _now;
                    continue;
                }
            }

            var managed = _engine.Businesses.Any(b => b.HasManager);
            var income = managed ? _engine.IncomePerSecond : ClickIncome(_engine.Businesses);
            var choice = Choose(income, managed);

            if (!managed)
            {
                if (choice is not null && _engine.Cash >= choice.Value.Cost)
                {
                    Execute(choice.Value);
                    continue;
                }
                foreach (var b in _engine.Businesses) _engine.StartBusiness(b.Id);
                _engine.Tick(ClickIntervalSeconds);
                _now += ClickIntervalSeconds;
                continue;
            }

            if (choice is null)
            {
                Advance(horizonSeconds - _now);
                break;
            }

            var cost = choice.Value.Cost;
            if (_engine.Cash < cost)
            {
                var wait = ((cost - _engine.Cash) / income).ToDouble();
                if (!double.IsFinite(wait) || _now + wait > horizonSeconds)
                {
                    Advance(horizonSeconds - _now);
                    break;
                }
                Advance(Math.Max(wait * 1.000001, MinimumJumpSeconds));
                if (_engine.Cash < cost) continue;
            }
            Execute(choice.Value);
        }

        while (samples.Count > 0 && samples.Peek() <= _now)
        {
            samples.Dequeue();
            _samples.Add((_now, Log10(_engine.IncomePerSecond)));
        }

        return new BalanceReport(_firstManager, _prestiges.ToList(), _samples.ToList(), _now, _steps);
    }

    private void Advance(double seconds)
    {
        if (seconds <= 0) return;
        if (seconds >= MinimumJumpSeconds) _engine.ApplyOfflineEarnings(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds));
        _now += seconds;
    }

    private void Execute((BigDouble Cost, double Score, string Id, long Count, bool Manager) choice)
    {
        if (choice.Count > 0 && _engine.BuyMultiple(choice.Id, choice.Count) < choice.Count) return;
        if (choice.Manager && _engine.BuyManager(choice.Id)) _firstManager ??= _now;
    }

    private (BigDouble Cost, double Score, string Id, long Count, bool Manager)? Choose(BigDouble income, bool managed)
    {
        var businesses = _engine.Businesses;
        var bonus = _engine.AngelBonus;
        var minOwned = _engine.MinOwnedAcrossBusinesses;
        var cross = CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.CalculateSpeedMultiplier(minOwned);
        var baseSum = BigDouble.Zero;
        foreach (var b in businesses)
            baseSum += managed ? (b.HasManager && b.Owned > 0 ? b.RevenuePerSecond : BigDouble.Zero) : ClickRate(b);

        (BigDouble Cost, double Score, string Id, long Count, bool Manager)? best = null;
        var cash = _engine.Cash;

        foreach (var b in businesses)
        {
            foreach (var target in Targets(b, minOwned))
            {
                var count = target - b.Owned;
                if (count <= 0) continue;
                var cost = b.CostOf(count);
                var after = b with { Owned = target };
                var newMin = businesses.Min(x => x.Id == b.Id ? target : x.Owned);
                var newCross = CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.CalculateSpeedMultiplier(newMin);
                var withManager = managed && !b.HasManager;
                if (withManager) cost += GameBalance.ManagerCost(b);
                var before = managed ? (b.HasManager ? b.RevenuePerSecond : BigDouble.Zero) : ClickRate(b);
                var gained = managed ? after.RevenuePerSecond : ClickRate(after);
                var newIncome = (baseSum - before + gained) * newCross * bonus;
                Consider(ref best, cost, newIncome - income, income, cash, b.Id, count, withManager);
            }

            if (!b.HasManager && b.Owned > 0)
            {
                var gain = (managed ? b.RevenuePerSecond : b.RevenuePerSecond - ClickRate(b)) * cross * bonus;
                Consider(ref best, GameBalance.ManagerCost(b), gain, income, cash, b.Id, 0, true);
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    private static void Consider(ref (BigDouble Cost, double Score, string Id, long Count, bool Manager)? best,
        BigDouble cost, BigDouble gain, BigDouble income, BigDouble cash, string id, long count, bool manager)
    {
        if (gain.Sign <= 0) return;
        var payback = (cost / gain).ToDouble();
        var wait = cash >= cost ? 0.0 : income.Sign > 0 ? ((cost - cash) / income).ToDouble() : double.PositiveInfinity;
        var score = wait + payback;
        if (!double.IsFinite(score)) return;
        if (best is null || score < best.Value.Score) best = (cost, score, id, count, manager);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<long> Targets(Business b, long minOwned)
    {
        var owned = b.Owned;
        var doubling = Math.Max(1L, (long)Math.Round(Math.Log(2.0) / Math.Log(b.CostMultiplier)));
        yield return owned < 25 ? owned + 1 : owned + doubling;
        var rev = Milestone.NextMilestone(owned);
        if (rev is not null) yield return rev.Threshold;
        var spd = SpeedMilestone.NextSpeedMilestone(owned);
        if (spd is not null) yield return spd.Threshold;
        if (owned == minOwned)
        {
            var cross = CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.NextThreshold(minOwned);
            if (cross is not null) yield return cross.Value;
        }
    }

    private static BigDouble ClickRate(Business b)
    {
        if (b.Owned <= 0) return BigDouble.Zero;
        return b.Revenue / new BigDouble(Math.Max(b.CycleTimeSeconds, ClickIntervalSeconds));
    }

    private static BigDouble ClickIncome(IReadOnlyList<Business> businesses)
    {
        var total = BigDouble.Zero;
        foreach (var b in businesses) total += ClickRate(b);
        return total;
    }

    private static double Log10(BigDouble value) => value.Sign > 0 ? value.Log10() : double.NegativeInfinity;
}

using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Tests.Balance;

public class FreshStartBalanceTests
{
    private static readonly BalanceReport Report = new BalanceBot().Play(horizonSeconds: 86_400, maxPrestiges: 10);

    [Fact]
    public void FirstManager_ArrivesWithinTwoMinutes()
    {
        (Report.FirstManagerSeconds ?? double.PositiveInfinity).ShouldBeLessThan(120.0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FirstPrestige_IsWorthDoublingIncome_WithinAnHour()
    {
        Report.Prestiges.Count.ShouldBeGreaterThan(0);
        Report.Prestiges[0].AtSeconds.ShouldBeInRange(8 * 60.0, 60 * 60.0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PrestigeLoop_NeitherRunsAwayNorStalls()
    {
        Report.Prestiges.Count.ShouldBe(10);
        foreach (var p in Report.Prestiges)
            p.RunSeconds.ShouldBeInRange(5 * 60.0, 90 * 60.0, $"prestige #{p.Number}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryPrestige_RaisesLifetimeEarnings()
    {
        for (var i = 1; i < Report.Prestiges.Count; i++)
            Report.Prestiges[i].LifetimeLog10.ShouldBeGreaterThan(Report.Prestiges[i - 1].LifetimeLog10);
    }
}

using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Tests.Balance;

public class LateGameBalanceTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(40.0)]
    [InlineData(80.0)]
    public void SeededLateGame_PrestigeRunsStayInBand(double lifetimeLog10)
    {
        var report = new BalanceBot(seedLifetimeLog10: lifetimeLog10).Play(horizonSeconds: 30 * 86_400, maxPrestiges: 3);

        report.Prestiges.Count.ShouldBe(3);
        foreach (var p in report.Prestiges)
        {
            p.RunSeconds.ShouldBeInRange(3 * 60.0, 4 * 3600.0, $"prestige #{p.Number}");
            double.IsFinite(p.LifetimeLog10).ShouldBeTrue();
        }
    }
}

using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Tests.Balance;

public class NeverPrestigeBalanceTests
{
    private const double Hour = 3600.0;
    private const double Day = 24 * Hour;

    private static readonly BalanceReport Report =
        new BalanceBot(prestigeAtIncomeMultiplier: 0).Play(7 * Day, sampleAt: [3 * Hour, Day, 7 * Day]);

    private static double IncomeAt(double seconds) =>
        Report.IncomeSamples.First(s => s.AtSeconds >= seconds).IncomeLog10;

    [Fact]
    public void Income_KeepsGrowingWithoutPrestige()
    {
        Report.IncomeSamples.Count.ShouldBe(3);
        (IncomeAt(Day) - IncomeAt(3 * Hour)).ShouldBeGreaterThan(1.0);
        (IncomeAt(7 * Day) - IncomeAt(Day)).ShouldBeGreaterThan(0.3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void FirstDay_ReachesTrillionsPerSecond() =>
        IncomeAt(Day).ShouldBeGreaterThan(12.0);
}

FILE: tests/MyAdventure.Core.Tests/BigDoubleInterfaceTests.cs
SIZE: 2.20 KB

using System.Globalization;
using System.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Core.Tests;

public class BigDoubleInterfaceTests
{
    private static T Sum<T>(IEnumerable<T> values) where T : IAdditionOperators<T, T, T>, IAdditiveIdentity<T, T>
    {
        var total = T.AdditiveIdentity;
        foreach (var value in values) total += value;
        return total;
    }

    private static T Max<T>(T a, T b) where T : IComparisonOperators<T, T, bool> => a > b ? a : b;

    [Fact]
    public void GenericMath_SumsThroughStaticAbstractMembers() =>
        Sum(new[] { new BigDouble(1), new BigDouble(2.5), new BigDouble(1.0, 3) }).ToDouble().ShouldBe(1003.5, tolerance: 1e-9);

    [Fact]
    public void GenericMath_ComparesThroughStaticAbstractMembers() =>
        Max(new BigDouble(1.0, 400), new BigDouble(9.0, 399)).ShouldBe(new BigDouble(1.0, 400));

    [Fact]
    public void Identities_AreZeroAndOne()
    {
        BigDouble.AdditiveIdentity.ShouldBe(BigDouble.Zero);
        BigDouble.MultiplicativeIdentity.ShouldBe(BigDouble.One);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SpanParsable_ParsesCanonicalAndPlainForms()
    {
        BigDouble.Parse("2.5e700".AsSpan(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ShouldBe(new BigDouble(2.5, 700));
        BigDouble.TryParse("1234.5".AsSpan(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var plain).ShouldBeTrue();
        plain.ShouldBe(new BigDouble(1234.5));
        BigDouble.TryParse("nope".AsSpan(), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out _).ShouldBeFalse();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SpanFormattable_InterpolatesCanonicalForm()
    {
        var value = new BigDouble(6.25, 1234);
        $"{value}".ShouldBe("6.25e1234");

        Span<char> tooSmall = stackalloc char[3];
        value.TryFormat(tooSmall, out var written, default, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ShouldBeFalse();
        written.ShouldBe(0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Comparable_SortsMixedMagnitudes()
    {
        BigDouble[] values = [new(1.0, 300), BigDouble.Zero, new(-5), BigDouble.PositiveInfinity, new(3)];

        Array.Sort(values);

        values.ShouldBe(new[] { new BigDouble(-5), BigDouble.Zero, new BigDouble(3), new BigDouble(1.0, 300), BigDouble.PositiveInfinity });
    }
}
