using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SchemaMigrationTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly SqliteTestDatabase _database = new();

    private static CancellationToken Ct => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_FreshDatabase_CreatesCurrentSchema()
    {
        await _database.InitializeAsync();

        using var connection = _database.OpenConnection();
        var columns = await SchemaUpgrader.GetColumnNamesAsync(connection, "GameStates", Ct);
        columns.ShouldContain("CashText");
        columns.ShouldContain("LifetimeEarningsText");
        columns.ShouldContain("AngelInvestorsText");
        columns.ShouldNotContain("Cash");
        (await SchemaUpgrader.TableExistsAsync(connection, "PlayerSettings", Ct)).ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_LegacyRealSchema_MigratesDataAndAddsSettings()
    {
        await CreateLegacyDatabaseAsync(cash: 1234.5, lifetime: 9999.99, angels: 50.0);

        await _database.InitializeAsync();

        using var connection = _database.OpenConnection();
        var columns = await SchemaUpgrader.GetColumnNamesAsync(connection, "GameStates", Ct);
        columns.ShouldContain("CashText");
        columns.ShouldNotContain("Cash");
        (await SchemaUpgrader.TableExistsAsync(connection, "PlayerSettings", Ct)).ShouldBeTrue();

        var snapshot = await _database.Get<IGameStateRepository>().LoadAsync(Ct);
        snapshot.ShouldNotBeNull();
        snapshot.Cash.ToDouble().ShouldBe(1234.5, tolerance: 1e-9);
        snapshot.LifetimeEarnings.ToDouble().ShouldBe(9999.99, tolerance: 1e-9);
        snapshot.AngelInvestors.ToDouble().ShouldBe(50.0, tolerance: 1e-9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_TextSchemaWithoutSettings_AddsSettingsAndKeepsProgress()
    {
        await CreatePreSettingsDatabaseAsync();

        await _database.InitializeAsync();

        var settings = await _database.Get<ISettingsRepository>().LoadAsync(Ct);
        settings.SoundEnabled.ShouldBeTrue();

        var snapshot = await _database.Get<IGameStateRepository>().LoadAsync(Ct);
        snapshot.ShouldNotBeNull();
        snapshot.Cash.ToCanonicalString().ShouldBe("4.2e100");
        snapshot.Owned["lemonade"].ShouldBe(7);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_RunTwice_IsIdempotent()
    {
        await _database.InitializeAsync();
        await new DatabaseInitializer(
                _database.Get<Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.IDbContextFactory<AppDbContext>>(),
                Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions.NullLogger<DatabaseInitializer>.Instance)
            .InitializeAsync(Ct);

        using var connection = _database.OpenConnection();
        (await SchemaUpgrader.GetColumnNamesAsync(connection, "GameStates", Ct)).ShouldContain("CashText");
    }

    private async Task CreateLegacyDatabaseAsync(double cash, double lifetime, double angels)
    {
        using var connection = _database.OpenConnection();
        await ExecAsync(connection, """
            CREATE TABLE GameStates (
                Id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
                CreatedAt TEXT NOT NULL,
                UpdatedAt TEXT NOT NULL,
                Cash REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                LifetimeEarnings REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                AngelInvestors REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                PrestigeCount INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                BusinessDataJson TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                ManagerDataJson TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                LastPlayedAt TEXT NOT NULL
            )
            """);

        await using var insert = connection.CreateCommand();
        insert.CommandText = """
            INSERT INTO GameStates (Id, CreatedAt, UpdatedAt, Cash, LifetimeEarnings, AngelInvestors, PrestigeCount, BusinessDataJson, ManagerDataJson, LastPlayedAt)
            VALUES ($id, $now, $now, $cash, $lt, $angels, 0, '{}', '{}', $now)
            """;
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("$id", Guid.NewGuid().ToString());
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("$now", DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"));
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("$cash", cash);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("$lt", lifetime);
        insert.Parameters.AddWithValue("$angels", angels);
        await insert.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(Ct);
    }

    private async Task CreatePreSettingsDatabaseAsync()
    {
        using var connection = _database.OpenConnection();
        await ExecAsync(connection, """
            CREATE TABLE "GameStates" (
                "Id" TEXT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT "PK_GameStates" PRIMARY KEY,
                "AngelInvestorsText" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                "BusinessDataJson" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                "CashText" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                "CreatedAt" TEXT NOT NULL,
                "LastPlayedAt" TEXT NOT NULL,
                "LifetimeEarningsText" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                "ManagerDataJson" TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                "PrestigeCount" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                "UpdatedAt" TEXT NOT NULL
            )
            """);
        await ExecAsync(connection, $$"""
            INSERT INTO GameStates (Id, AngelInvestorsText, BusinessDataJson, CashText, CreatedAt, LastPlayedAt, LifetimeEarningsText, ManagerDataJson, PrestigeCount, UpdatedAt)
            VALUES ('{{Guid.NewGuid()}}', '0', '{"lemonade":7}', '4.2e100', '2026-01-01 00:00:00', '2026-01-01 00:00:00', '4.2e100', '{"lemonade":false}', 0, '2026-01-01 00:00:00')
            """);
    }

    private static async Task ExecAsync(SqliteConnection connection, string sql)
    {
        await using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
        command.CommandText = sql;
        await command.ExecuteNonQueryAsync(Ct);
    }

    public void Dispose() => _database.Dispose();
}


================================================================================
FILE: tests/MyAdventure.Integration.Tests/SentryEnvelopeTests.cs

using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Integration.Tests;

public class SentryEnvelopeTests
{
    private const string TestDsn =
        "https://abc123@o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/4511444969390160";

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_EnvelopeEndpoint_HasExpectedShape()
    {
        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);

        parsed.EnvelopeEndpoint.ShouldBe(
            "https://o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/api/4511444969390160/envelope/");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_HasThreeNewlineDelimitedJsonLines()
    {
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var payload = MakePayload(new InvalidOperationException("test boom"));

        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            payload, dsn,
            serviceName: "MyAdventure",
            serviceVersion: "1.2.3",
            environment: "test",
            now: new DateTimeOffset(2026, 5, 27, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero));

        var text = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(bytes);
        var parts = text.Split('\n');
        parts.Length.ShouldBeGreaterThanOrEqualTo(3);
        parts[0].ShouldNotBeNullOrEmpty();
        parts[1].ShouldNotBeNullOrEmpty();
        parts[2].ShouldNotBeNullOrEmpty();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_HeaderCarriesDsnEventIdAndSentAt()
    {
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            MakePayload(new Exception("x")), dsn,
            "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "production",
            new DateTimeOffset(2026, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, TimeSpan.Zero));

        var header = ParseHeader(bytes);

        header.GetProperty("event_id").GetString().ShouldNotBeNullOrEmpty();
        header.GetProperty("dsn").GetString().ShouldBe(TestDsn);
        header.GetProperty("sent_at").GetString()
            .ShouldStartWith("2026-01-02T03:04:05");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_ItemHeaderDeclaresEventTypeAndLength()
    {
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            MakePayload(new Exception("x")), dsn,
            "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "production", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var (_, itemHeader, payload) = ParseAllThree(bytes);

        itemHeader.GetProperty("type").GetString().ShouldBe("event");
        itemHeader.GetProperty("content_type").GetString().ShouldBe("application/json");
        itemHeader.GetProperty("length").GetInt32()
            .ShouldBe(payload.Bytes.Length);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_EventPayloadCarriesExceptionInterface()
    {
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var ex = MakeRealException("boom!");

        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            MakePayload(ex), dsn,
            "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "production", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var (_, _, payload) = ParseAllThree(bytes);
        var exceptionInterface = payload.Json.GetProperty("exception");
        var values = exceptionInterface.GetProperty("values");

        values.GetArrayLength().ShouldBe(1);
        var first = values[0];
        first.GetProperty("type").GetString().ShouldBe(typeof(InvalidOperationException).FullName);
        first.GetProperty("value").GetString().ShouldBe("boom!");

        var frames = first.GetProperty("stacktrace").GetProperty("frames");
        frames.GetArrayLength().ShouldBeGreaterThan(0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_EventPayloadCarriesEnvironmentAndRelease()
    {
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            MakePayload(new Exception("x")), dsn,
            "MyAdventure", "9.8.7", "staging", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var (_, _, payload) = ParseAllThree(bytes);

        payload.Json.GetProperty("environment").GetString().ShouldBe("staging");
        payload.Json.GetProperty("release").GetString().ShouldBe("MyAdventure@9.8.7");
        payload.Json.GetProperty("platform").GetString().ShouldBe("csharp");
        payload.Json.GetProperty("level").GetString().ShouldBe("error");
        payload.Json.GetProperty("logger").GetString().ShouldBe("MyAdventure.TestCategory");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Envelope_InnerExceptionsAreIncluded()
    {
        var inner = MakeRealException("inner");
        var outer = new InvalidOperationException("outer", inner);

        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);
        var bytes = SentryEnvelope.BuildExceptionEvent(
            MakePayload(outer), dsn,
            "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "test", DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);

        var (_, _, payload) = ParseAllThree(bytes);
        var values = payload.Json.GetProperty("exception").GetProperty("values");

        values.GetArrayLength().ShouldBe(2);
        values[0].GetProperty("value").GetString().ShouldBe("inner");
        values[1].GetProperty("value").GetString().ShouldBe("outer");
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Trace, false, false)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Debug, false, false)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Information, false, false)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Warning, false, false)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Error, false, false)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Critical, false, true)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Trace, true, true)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Information, true, true)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Error, true, true)]
    [InlineData(LogLevel.Critical, true, true)]
    public void ShouldPromoteToIssue_FollowsExceptionFirstRule(
        LogLevel level, bool hasException, bool expected)
    {
        var exception = hasException ? new InvalidOperationException("x") : null;

        SentryEventLoggerProvider
            .ShouldPromoteToIssue(level, exception)
            .ShouldBe(expected);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Provider_LogErrorWithException_PostsEnvelopeToSentry()
    {
        var capture = new CapturingHandler();
        using var http = new HttpClient(capture);
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);

        using var provider = new SentryEventLoggerProvider(
            dsn, http,
            serviceName: "MyAdventure",
            serviceVersion: "1.0.0",
            environment: "test",
            timeProvider: new FixedTimeProvider(
                new DateTimeOffset(2026, 5, 27, 12, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero)));

        var logger = provider.CreateLogger("MyAdventure.GameEngine");
        logger.LogError(MakeRealException("kaboom"), "something failed: {Detail}", "details");

        var request = await capture.AwaitRequestAsync(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2));

        request.ShouldNotBeNull();
        request!.Method.ShouldBe(HttpMethod.Post);
        request.RequestUri!.ToString().ShouldBe(dsn.EnvelopeEndpoint);

        request.Headers.TryGetValues("X-Sentry-Auth", out var authValues).ShouldBeTrue();
        string.Join(',', authValues!).ShouldBe(dsn.AuthHeaderValue);

        var bodyBytes = capture.RequestBody!;
        var (envHeader, itemHeader, eventPayload) = ParseAllThree(bodyBytes);

        envHeader.GetProperty("dsn").GetString().ShouldBe(TestDsn);
        itemHeader.GetProperty("type").GetString().ShouldBe("event");
        eventPayload.Json.GetProperty("exception")
            .GetProperty("values")[0]
            .GetProperty("value").GetString().ShouldBe("kaboom");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Provider_LogInformation_DoesNotPost()
    {
        var capture = new CapturingHandler();
        using var http = new HttpClient(capture);
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);

        using var provider = new SentryEventLoggerProvider(
            dsn, http, "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "test", new FixedTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));

        var logger = provider.CreateLogger("MyAdventure.GameEngine");
        logger.LogInformation("benign info message");

        await Task.Delay(100, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        capture.RequestCount.ShouldBe(0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Provider_HttpFailure_DoesNotThrowToCaller()
    {
        var failing = new ThrowingHandler();
        using var http = new HttpClient(failing);
        var dsn = SentryDsn.Parse(TestDsn);

        using var provider = new SentryEventLoggerProvider(
            dsn, http, "MyAdventure", "1.0.0", "test", new FixedTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow));

        var logger = provider.CreateLogger("MyAdventure.GameEngine");
        Should.NotThrow(() => logger.LogError(new Exception("x"), "x"));

        await Task.Delay(50, TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);
    }

    private static InvalidOperationException MakeRealException(string message)
    {
        try
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
        {
            return ex;
        }
    }

    private static SentryEventPayload MakePayload(Exception ex) =>
        new(ex, "test message", "error", "MyAdventure.TestCategory");

    private static JsonElement ParseHeader(byte[] envelopeBytes)
    {
        var newlineIndex = Array.IndexOf(envelopeBytes, (byte)'\n');
        newlineIndex.ShouldBeGreaterThan(0);
        var slice = new byte[newlineIndex];
        Array.Copy(envelopeBytes, slice, newlineIndex);
        return JsonDocument.Parse(slice).RootElement.Clone();
    }

    private record ParsedPayload(JsonElement Json, byte[] Bytes);

    private static (JsonElement EnvHeader, JsonElement ItemHeader, ParsedPayload Payload)
        ParseAllThree(byte[] envelopeBytes)
    {
        var parts = new List<byte[]>();
        var start = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < envelopeBytes.Length && parts.Count < 3; i++)
        {
            if (envelopeBytes[i] == (byte)'\n')
            {
                var chunk = new byte[i - start];
                Array.Copy(envelopeBytes, start, chunk, 0, chunk.Length);
                if (chunk.Length > 0) parts.Add(chunk);
                start = i + 1;
            }
        }
        parts.Count.ShouldBe(3, "envelope must have 3 JSON lines");

        var envHeader = JsonDocument.Parse(parts[0]).RootElement.Clone();
        var itemHeader = JsonDocument.Parse(parts[1]).RootElement.Clone();
        var payloadJson = JsonDocument.Parse(parts[2]).RootElement.Clone();
        return (envHeader, itemHeader, new ParsedPayload(payloadJson, parts[2]));
    }

    private sealed class CapturingHandler : HttpMessageHandler
    {
        private readonly TaskCompletionSource<HttpRequestMessage> _signal =
            new(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);

        public byte[]? RequestBody { get; private set; }
        public int RequestCount { get; private set; }

        protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(
            HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            RequestCount++;
            if (request.Content is not null)
            {
                RequestBody = await request.Content.ReadAsByteArrayAsync(cancellationToken);
            }
            _signal.TrySetResult(request);
            return new HttpResponseMessage(HttpStatusCode.OK);
        }

        public async Task<HttpRequestMessage?> AwaitRequestAsync(TimeSpan timeout)
        {
            var winner = await Task.WhenAny(_signal.Task, Task.Delay(timeout));
            return winner == _signal.Task ? await _signal.Task : null;
        }
    }

    private sealed class ThrowingHandler : HttpMessageHandler
    {
        protected override Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(
            HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
            => throw new HttpRequestException("simulated network failure");
    }

    private sealed class FixedTimeProvider : TimeProvider
    {
        private readonly DateTimeOffset _now;
        public FixedTimeProvider(DateTimeOffset now) => _now = now;
        public override DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => _now;
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SettingsRepositoryTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly SqliteTestDatabase _database = new();

    private static CancellationToken Ct => TestContext.Current.CancellationToken;

    [Fact]
    public async Task Load_NothingSaved_ReturnsDefaults()
    {
        var settings = await _database.Get<ISettingsRepository>().LoadAsync(Ct);

        settings.ShouldBe(PlayerSettings.Default);
        settings.SoundEnabled.ShouldBeTrue();
        settings.HapticsEnabled.ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task SaveAndLoad_RoundTrips()
    {
        var repo = _database.Get<ISettingsRepository>();

        await repo.SaveAsync(new PlayerSettings(SoundEnabled: false, HapticsEnabled: true), Ct);
        await repo.SaveAsync(new PlayerSettings(SoundEnabled: false, HapticsEnabled: false), Ct);

        (await repo.LoadAsync(Ct)).ShouldBe(new PlayerSettings(false, false));
    }

    public void Dispose() => _database.Dispose();
}

using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;

namespace MyAdventure.Integration.Tests;

public sealed class SqliteTestDatabase : IDisposable
{
    public SqliteTestDatabase()
    {
        Path = System.IO.Path.Combine(System.IO.Path.GetTempPath(), $"myadventure-test-{Guid.NewGuid():N}.db");
        Services = new ServiceCollection().AddInfrastructure(Path).BuildServiceProvider();
    }

    public string Path { get; }

    public ServiceProvider Services { get; }

    public T Get<T>() where T : notnull => Services.GetRequiredService<T>();

    public Task InitializeAsync() => Get<DatabaseInitializer>().InitializeAsync(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

    public SqliteConnection OpenConnection()
    {
        var connection = new SqliteConnection($"Data Source={Path}");
        connection.Open();
        return connection;
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        Services.Dispose();
        SqliteConnection.ClearAllPools();
        try
        {
            File.Delete(Path);
        }
        catch (IOException)
        {
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Data;
using MyAdventure.Infrastructure.Telemetry;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.Integration.Tests;

public class TelemetryConfigurationTests : IDisposable
{
    private readonly string _dbPath;
    private readonly List<string> _envVarsToRestore = new();

    public TelemetryConfigurationTests()
    {
        _dbPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"myadventure-test-{Guid.NewGuid():N}.db");

        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.SentryDsnEnvVar, null);
        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.VerboseLoggingEnvVar, null);
        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.SentryEnvironmentEnvVar, null);
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        foreach (var name in _envVarsToRestore)
        {
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(name, null);
        }

        if (File.Exists(_dbPath))
        {
            try { File.Delete(_dbPath); } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static IConfiguration EmptyConfiguration => new ConfigurationBuilder().Build();

    private void SetEnv(string name, string? value)
    {
        if (!_envVarsToRestore.Contains(name)) _envVarsToRestore.Add(name);
        Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(name, value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_TryParse_RealisticDsn_PopulatesAllFields()
    {
        const string dsn =
            "https://fe6ae5ee15285c313b8171bb7a5a4ad0@o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/4511444969390160";

        var ok = SentryDsn.TryParse(dsn, out var parsed, out var err);

        ok.ShouldBeTrue(err);
        parsed.ShouldNotBeNull();
        parsed.PublicKey.ShouldBe("fe6ae5ee15285c313b8171bb7a5a4ad0");
        parsed.ProjectId.ShouldBe("4511444969390160");
        parsed.Host.ShouldBe("o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_TracesEndpoint_HasExpectedShape()
    {
        const string dsn =
            "https://fe6ae5ee15285c313b8171bb7a5a4ad0@o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/4511444969390160";

        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(dsn);

        parsed.TracesEndpoint.ToString().ShouldBe(
            "https://o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/api/4511444969390160/integration/otlp/v1/traces");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_LogsEndpoint_HasExpectedShape()
    {
        const string dsn =
            "https://fe6ae5ee15285c313b8171bb7a5a4ad0@o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/4511444969390160";

        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(dsn);

        parsed.LogsEndpoint.ToString().ShouldBe(
            "https://o4511444968079360.ingest.de.sentry.io/api/4511444969390160/integration/otlp/v1/logs");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_AuthHeader_MatchesSentryDocumentedFormat()
    {
        const string dsn =
            "https://abc123@o123.ingest.us.sentry.io/456";

        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(dsn);

        parsed.AuthHeaderValue.ShouldBe("sentry sentry_key=abc123");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_AuthHeader_ContainsNoComma_RegressionForOtlpHeaderSplit()
    {
        const string modernDsn =
            "https://abc123@o123.ingest.us.sentry.io/456";
        const string legacyDsn =
            "https://abc123:secret456@o123.ingest.us.sentry.io/789";

        SentryDsn.Parse(modernDsn).AuthHeaderValue.ShouldNotContain(",");
        SentryDsn.Parse(legacyDsn).AuthHeaderValue.ShouldNotContain(",");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_AuthHeader_LegacySecretKeyIncluded()
    {
        const string dsn = "https://pubkey:secret@example.com/9";

        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(dsn);

        parsed.AuthHeaderValue.ShouldBe(
            "sentry sentry_key=pubkey sentry_secret=secret");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SentryDsn_TryParse_HandlesLegacyPublicSecretKeyFormat()
    {
        const string dsn = "https://pubkey:potato@example.com/9";
        var parsed = SentryDsn.Parse(dsn);
        parsed.PublicKey.ShouldBe("pubkey");
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("")]
    [InlineData("   ")]
    [InlineData(null)]
    [InlineData("not-a-url")]
    [InlineData("ftp://x@example.com/1")]
    [InlineData("https://example.com/1")]
    [InlineData("https://potato@example.com")]
    public void SentryDsn_TryParse_RejectsInvalidInput(string? dsn)
    {
        var ok = SentryDsn.TryParse(dsn, out var parsed, out var err);
        ok.ShouldBeFalse();
        parsed.ShouldBeNull();
        err.ShouldNotBeNullOrWhiteSpace();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_NoFallback_NoVarsSet_ReturnsSafeDefaults()
    {
        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(EmptyConfiguration, fallbackDsn: "");

        options.VerboseLogging.ShouldBeFalse();
        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBeNullOrEmpty();
        options.Sentry.Environment.ShouldBe("production");
        options.Sentry.TracesSampleRate.ShouldBe(1.0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_NoFallback_VerboseEnvVar_Wins()
    {
        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.VerboseLoggingEnvVar, "true");

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(EmptyConfiguration, fallbackDsn: "");

        options.VerboseLogging.ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData("1", true)]
    [InlineData("true", true)]
    [InlineData("TRUE", true)]
    [InlineData("yes", true)]
    [InlineData("on", true)]
    [InlineData("0", false)]
    [InlineData("false", false)]
    [InlineData("off", false)]
    [InlineData("nope", false)]
    public void Loader_VerboseFlag_ParsesCommonBooleanSpellings(string raw, bool expected)
    {
        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.VerboseLoggingEnvVar, raw);
        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(EmptyConfiguration, fallbackDsn: "");
        options.VerboseLogging.ShouldBe(expected);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_NoFallback_BindsJsonShape()
    {
        var json = new Dictionary<string, string?>
        {
            ["Telemetry:VerboseLogging"] = "true",
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn"] = "https://potato@example.com/1",
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Environment"] = "staging",
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:TracesSampleRate"] = "0.25",
        };
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(json).Build();

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(config, fallbackDsn: "");

        options.VerboseLogging.ShouldBeTrue();
        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe("https://potato@example.com/1");
        options.Sentry.Environment.ShouldBe("staging");
        options.Sentry.TracesSampleRate.ShouldBe(0.25);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_EnvironmentVariables_OverrideJsonValues()
    {
        var json = new Dictionary<string, string?>
        {
            ["Telemetry:VerboseLogging"] = "false",
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn"] = "https://potato@example.com/1",
        };
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(json).Build();

        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.SentryDsnEnvVar, "https://potato@example.com/2");
        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.VerboseLoggingEnvVar, "true");

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(config, fallbackDsn: "");

        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe("https://potato@example.com/2");
        options.VerboseLogging.ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    private const string TestFallback = "https://potato@example.com/9";

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_WithFallback_NoVarsSet_UsesFallbackDsn()
    {
        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(EmptyConfiguration, TestFallback);

        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe(TestFallback);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_WithFallback_AppsettingsEmptyDsn_UsesFallback()
    {
        var json = new Dictionary<string, string?>
        {
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn"] = "",
        };
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(json).Build();

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(config, TestFallback);

        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe(TestFallback);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_WithFallback_AppsettingsNonEmptyDsn_OverridesFallback()
    {
        var json = new Dictionary<string, string?>
        {
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn"] = "https://potato@example.com/1",
        };
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(json).Build();

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(config, TestFallback);

        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe("https://potato@example.com/1");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Loader_WithFallback_EnvVar_OverridesEverything()
    {
        var json = new Dictionary<string, string?>
        {
            ["Telemetry:Sentry:Dsn"] = "https://potato@example.com/1",
        };
        var config = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(json).Build();

        SetEnv(TelemetryConfigurationLoader.SentryDsnEnvVar, "https://potato@example.com/2");

        var options = TelemetryConfigurationLoader.LoadFromConfiguration(config, TestFallback);

        options.Sentry.Dsn.ShouldBe("https://potato@example.com/2");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TelemetryDefaults_DefaultDsn_IsAValidParseableSentryDsn()
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(TelemetryDefaults.DefaultDsn))
        {
            return;
        }

        var ok = SentryDsn.TryParse(TelemetryDefaults.DefaultDsn, out var parsed, out var err);

        ok.ShouldBeTrue(err);
        parsed.ShouldNotBeNull();
        parsed.PublicKey.ShouldNotBeNullOrWhiteSpace();
        parsed.ProjectId.ShouldNotBeNullOrWhiteSpace();
        parsed.Host.ShouldNotBeNullOrWhiteSpace();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddInfrastructure_NoTelemetryOptions_BehavesLikeBeforeIntegration()
    {
        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        services.AddInfrastructure(_dbPath);
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();

        await provider.GetRequiredService<DatabaseInitializer>().InitializeAsync(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        provider.GetService<IGameStateRepository>().ShouldNotBeNull();
        provider.GetService<ILoggerFactory>().ShouldNotBeNull();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddInfrastructure_TelemetryOff_NoOutboundExporterErrors()
    {
        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        services.AddInfrastructure(new TelemetryOptions(), _dbPath);
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();

        TelemetryLifecycle.Start(provider);
        await provider.GetRequiredService<DatabaseInitializer>().InitializeAsync(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        provider.GetService<TelemetryOptions>().ShouldNotBeNull();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddInfrastructure_TelemetryOnWithValidDsn_ProviderStillBuilds()
    {
        var telemetry = new TelemetryOptions
        {
            VerboseLogging = false,
            Sentry =
            {
                Dsn = "https://potato@example.com/2",
                Environment = "test",
                TracesSampleRate = 1.0,
            },
        };

        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        services.AddInfrastructure(telemetry, _dbPath);
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();

        TelemetryLifecycle.Start(provider);
        await provider.GetRequiredService<DatabaseInitializer>().InitializeAsync(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        provider.GetService<IGameStateRepository>().ShouldNotBeNull();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task AddInfrastructure_TelemetryOnWithMalformedDsn_DoesNotCrash()
    {
        var telemetry = new TelemetryOptions
        {
            Sentry = { Dsn = "definitely not a url" },
        };

        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        services.AddInfrastructure(telemetry, _dbPath);
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();

        Should.NotThrow(() => TelemetryLifecycle.Start(provider));
        await provider.GetRequiredService<DatabaseInitializer>().InitializeAsync(TestContext.Current.CancellationToken);

        provider.GetService<IGameStateRepository>().ShouldNotBeNull();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AddInfrastructure_VerboseFlagPropagatesToTelemetryOptionsSingleton()
    {
        var telemetry = new TelemetryOptions { VerboseLogging = true };

        var services = new ServiceCollection();
        services.AddInfrastructure(telemetry, _dbPath);
        var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();

        var resolved = provider.GetRequiredService<TelemetryOptions>();
        resolved.VerboseLogging.ShouldBeTrue();
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using NSubstitute;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class BusinessViewModelFeedbackTests
{
    private readonly IFeedbackService _feedback = Substitute.For<IFeedbackService>();
    private readonly GameEngine _engine = Ui.CreateEngine();

    private BusinessViewModel Lemonade() =>
        Ui.CreateBusiness(_engine.Businesses.First(b => b.Id == "lemonade"), _engine, _feedback);

    [Fact]
    public void Buy_Affordable_PlaysPurchase()
    {
        _engine.Cash = new BigDouble(100);

        Lemonade().BuyBusinessCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Purchase);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Buy_Unaffordable_PlaysDenied()
    {
        _engine.Cash = BigDouble.Zero;

        Lemonade().BuyBusinessCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Buy_CrossingMilestone_PlaysMilestone()
    {
        _engine.Cash = new BigDouble(1.0, 9);
        _engine.Businesses.First(b => b.Id == "lemonade").Owned = 24;

        Lemonade().BuyBusinessCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Milestone);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BulkBuy_ReachingMilestone_PlaysMilestone()
    {
        _engine.Cash = new BigDouble(1.0, 9);

        Lemonade().BulkBuyCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Milestone);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Run_OwnedIdleBusiness_PlaysTap()
    {
        _engine.Cash = new BigDouble(100);
        _engine.BuyBusiness("lemonade");

        Lemonade().ClickBusinessCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Manager_Affordable_PlaysManager()
    {
        _engine.Cash = new BigDouble(1.0, 6);
        _engine.BuyBusiness("lemonade");

        Lemonade().BuyManagerCommand.Execute(null);

        _feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Manager);
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class BusinessViewModelSpeedTests
{
    private static readonly BigDouble NoAngels = BigDouble.One;

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_BelowFirstSpeedThreshold_HidesSpeedRow()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 24);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);

        vm.SpeedMultiplier.ShouldBe(1.0);
        vm.HasSpeedBonus.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.HasNextSpeedMilestone.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.NextSpeedMilestoneText.ShouldContain("25");
        vm.NextSpeedMilestoneText.ShouldContain("Speed");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_At25_ShowsTwoSpeed()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 25);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);

        vm.SpeedMultiplier.ShouldBe(2.0);
        vm.HasSpeedBonus.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.SpeedMultiplierText.ShouldBe("×2 Speed");
        vm.HasNextSpeedMilestone.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.NextSpeedMilestoneText.ShouldContain("50");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_At100_ShowsEightSpeed()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);

        vm.SpeedMultiplier.ShouldBe(8.0);
        vm.HasSpeedBonus.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.SpeedMultiplierText.ShouldBe("×8 Speed");
        vm.HasNextSpeedMilestone.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.NextSpeedMilestoneText.ShouldContain("200");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_At400_ShowsMaxSpeedAndNoNext()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 400);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1.0, 50), NoAngels);

        vm.SpeedMultiplier.ShouldBe(64.0);
        vm.HasSpeedBonus.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.SpeedMultiplierText.ShouldBe("×64 Speed");
        vm.HasNextSpeedMilestone.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.NextSpeedMilestoneText.ShouldBe("");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_CycleTimeText_ReflectsSpeedMultiplier()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        biz = biz with { BaseTimeSeconds = 0.6 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);

        vm.CycleTimeText.ShouldBe("75ms");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_RevenuePerSecondText_CompoundsRevenueAndSpeed()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 1.0, BaseTimeSeconds = 1.0, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);

        vm.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe("$6.40 K/s");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_WithCrossBusinessMultiplier_ScalesRevenueDisplay()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 1.0, BaseTimeSeconds = 1.0, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels, new BigDouble(2.0));

        vm.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe("$12.80 K/s");

        vm.CycleTimeText.ShouldBe("125ms");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_TwoArgOverload_EquivalentToThreeArgWithOne()
    {
        var biz1 = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        biz1 = biz1 with { BaseRevenue = 1.0, BaseTimeSeconds = 1.0, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm1, _) = MakeVm(biz1);

        var biz2 = MakeBusiness(owned: 100);
        biz2 = biz2 with { BaseRevenue = 1.0, BaseTimeSeconds = 1.0, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm2, _) = MakeVm(biz2);

        vm1.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels);
        vm2.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), NoAngels, BigDouble.One);

        vm2.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe(vm1.RevenuePerSecondText);
        vm2.RevenueText.ShouldBe(vm1.RevenueText);
        vm2.CycleTimeText.ShouldBe(vm1.CycleTimeText);
        vm2.SpeedMultiplier.ShouldBe(vm1.SpeedMultiplier);
    }

    private static Business MakeBusiness(int owned = 0) => new()
    {
        Id = "test",
        Name = "Test",
        Icon = "T",
        Color = "#FFF",
        BaseCost = 1,
        BaseRevenue = 10,
        BaseTimeSeconds = 1,
        CostMultiplier = 1.01,
        Owned = owned
    };

    private static (BusinessViewModel vm, GameEngine engine) MakeVm(Business biz)
    {
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine();
        return (Ui.CreateBusiness(biz, engine), engine);
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class BusinessViewModelTests
{
    private static readonly BigDouble NoAngels = BigDouble.One;

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_ShouldUpdateAllProperties()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 3);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(500), NoAngels);

        vm.Owned.ShouldBe(3);
        vm.CostText.ShouldNotBeNullOrEmpty();
        vm.RevenueText.ShouldNotBe("—");
        vm.CanAfford.ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_NotEnoughCash_ShouldShowNotAffordable()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness();
        biz = biz with { BaseCost = 1000 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(5), NoAngels);
        vm.CanAfford.ShouldBeFalse();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_BeforeMilestone_ShowsBuyToMilestoneLabel()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 20);
        biz = biz with { BaseCost = 10, CostMultiplier = 1.05 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(10_000), NoAngels);

        vm.HasNextMilestone.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.BulkBuyText.ShouldBe("BUY 5→25");
        vm.CanBulkBuy.ShouldBeTrue();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_AllMilestonesReached_BulkBuyButtonStaysVisibleAsBuyMax()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 1000);
        biz = biz with { BaseCost = 10, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1.0, 50), NoAngels);

        vm.HasNextMilestone.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.CanBulkBuy.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.BulkBuyText.ShouldStartWith("BUY MAX");
        vm.BulkBuyText.ShouldContain("(");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_AllMilestonesReached_NoCash_BulkBuyDimsButStaysVisible()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 1000);
        biz = biz with { BaseCost = 10, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(BigDouble.Zero, NoAngels);

        vm.HasNextMilestone.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.CanBulkBuy.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.BulkBuyText.ShouldBe("BUY MAX");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_WithAngelBonus_ShouldMultiplyRevenueText()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 1);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 100, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), angelBonus: new BigDouble(2.0));

        vm.RevenueText.ShouldBe("200.00");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_WithAngelBonus_ShouldMultiplyRevenuePerSecondText()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 1);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 60, BaseTimeSeconds = 2, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), angelBonus: new BigDouble(3.0));

        vm.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe("$90.00/s");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_NoAngelBonus_ShouldShowBaseRevenue()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 1);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 100, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), angelBonus: BigDouble.One);

        vm.RevenueText.ShouldBe("100.00");
        vm.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe("$100.00/s");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_AngelBonusWithMilestones_ShouldStack()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 25);
        biz = biz with { BaseRevenue = 1, CostMultiplier = 1.01 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), angelBonus: new BigDouble(4.0));

        vm.RevenueText.ShouldBe("200.00");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_ZeroOwned_ShouldShowDashRegardlessOfAngelBonus()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 0);
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1_000_000), angelBonus: new BigDouble(50.0));

        vm.RevenueText.ShouldBe("—");
        vm.RevenuePerSecondText.ShouldBe("—");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Refresh_AtHugeCash_CostTextRemainsCompact()
    {
        var biz = MakeBusiness(owned: 5000);
        biz = biz with { BaseCost = 4, CostMultiplier = 1.07 };
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm(biz);

        vm.Refresh(new BigDouble(1.0, 500), NoAngels);

        vm.CostText.Length.ShouldBeLessThan(25);
        vm.CostText.ShouldNotContain("Infinity");
        vm.CostText.ShouldNotContain("NaN");
    }

    private static Business MakeBusiness(int owned = 0) => new()
    {
        Id = "test",
        Name = "Test",
        Icon = "T",
        Color = "#FFF",
        BaseCost = 100,
        BaseRevenue = 10,
        BaseTimeSeconds = 1,
        CostMultiplier = 1.1,
        Owned = owned
    };

    private static (BusinessViewModel vm, GameEngine engine) MakeVm(Business biz)
    {
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine();
        return (Ui.CreateBusiness(biz, engine), engine);
    }
}

FILE: tests/MyAdventure.UI.Tests/FeedbackServiceTests.cs

using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using NSubstitute;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class FeedbackServiceTests
{
    private readonly ISoundPlayer _sound = Substitute.For<ISoundPlayer>();
    private readonly IHapticPlayer _haptics = Substitute.For<IHapticPlayer>();

    public FeedbackServiceTests() => _haptics.IsSupported.Returns(true);

    private FeedbackService CreateService() => new(_sound, _haptics, NullLogger<FeedbackService>.Instance);

    [Fact]
    public void Play_BothEnabled_PlaysSoundAndHaptics()
    {
        CreateService().Play(FeedbackCue.Purchase);

        _sound.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Purchase);
        _haptics.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Purchase);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Play_SoundDisabled_OnlyPlaysHaptics()
    {
        var service = CreateService();
        service.SoundEnabled = false;

        service.Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);

        _sound.DidNotReceiveWithAnyArgs().Play(default);
        _haptics.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Play_HapticsDisabled_OnlyPlaysSound()
    {
        var service = CreateService();
        service.HapticsEnabled = false;

        service.Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);

        _sound.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);
        _haptics.DidNotReceiveWithAnyArgs().Play(default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Play_HapticsUnsupported_SkipsHaptics()
    {
        _haptics.IsSupported.Returns(false);
        var service = CreateService();

        service.Play(FeedbackCue.Milestone);

        service.HapticsSupported.ShouldBeFalse();
        _haptics.DidNotReceiveWithAnyArgs().Play(default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Play_PlayerThrows_IsSwallowed()
    {
        _sound.When(s => s.Play(Arg.Any<FeedbackCue>())).Do(_ => throw new InvalidOperationException("no audio device"));

        Should.NotThrow(() => CreateService().Play(FeedbackCue.Tap));
    }
}

FILE: tests/MyAdventure.UI.Tests/GameViewModelLifecycleTests.cs

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class GameViewModelLifecycleTests
{
    private static readonly double FiftyAngelBonus = 1.0 + GameBalance.AngelBonusPerAngel * 50;

    private sealed class TestTimeProvider : TimeProvider
    {
        private DateTimeOffset _now;
        public TestTimeProvider(DateTime utcStart) => _now = new DateTimeOffset(utcStart, TimeSpan.Zero);
        public override DateTimeOffset GetUtcNow() => _now;
        public void Advance(TimeSpan by) => _now = _now.Add(by);
    }

    private static (GameViewModel vm, GameEngine engine, TestTimeProvider clock, ToastService toasts) MakeVm(double angels = 0)
    {
        var clock = new TestTimeProvider(new DateTime(2025, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc));
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine(clock);
        var toasts = new ToastService();
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(engine, clock, toasts);

        if (angels > 0) engine.AngelInvestors = new BigDouble(angels);
        engine.Cash = new BigDouble(1_000_000.0);

        engine.BuyBusiness("lemonade");
        engine.BuyManager("lemonade");
        engine.Businesses.First(b => b.Id == "lemonade").ProgressPercent = 0;

        return (vm, engine, clock, toasts);
    }

    private static double LemonadeRevenue(GameEngine engine) =>
        engine.Businesses.First(b => b.Id == "lemonade").Revenue.ToDouble();

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_WithoutPriorSuspend_ShouldNotApplyEarnings()
    {
        var (vm, engine, _, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnResumed();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(cashBefore);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_TwiceWithoutSuspend_ShouldNotApplyEarnings()
    {
        var (vm, engine, _, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnResumed();
        vm.OnResumed();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(cashBefore);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnSuspendedThenOnResumed_ShouldApplyEarningsForGap()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10));
        vm.OnResumed();

        var earned = (engine.Cash - cashBefore).ToDouble();
        earned.ShouldBe(1000.0 * LemonadeRevenue(engine), tolerance: 1e-9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnSuspendedThenOnResumed_ShouldApplyAngelBonusOnce()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm(angels: 50);
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(60));
        vm.OnResumed();

        var earned = (engine.Cash - cashBefore).ToDouble();
        earned.ShouldBe(100.0 * LemonadeRevenue(engine) * FiftyAngelBonus, tolerance: 1e-7);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_ShouldShowToast_WhenEarningsApplied()
    {
        var (vm, _, clock, toasts) = MakeVm();

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
        vm.OnResumed();

        toasts.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(1);
        toasts.ActiveToasts[0].Message.ShouldContain("While you were away");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_ShouldNotShowToast_WhenNoEarnings()
    {
        var clock = new TestTimeProvider(new DateTime(2025, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc));
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine(clock);
        var toasts = new ToastService();
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(engine, clock, toasts);

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
        vm.OnResumed();

        toasts.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(0);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_TinyGap_ShouldNotApplyEarnings()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500));
        vm.OnResumed();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(cashBefore);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_AfterFirstResume_RequiresNewSuspend()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));
        vm.OnResumed();

        var cashAfterFirst = engine.Cash;
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));

        vm.OnResumed();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(cashAfterFirst);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_ShouldResetLastTickToCurrentTime()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10));
        vm.OnResumed();

        var cashAfterResume = engine.Cash;

        engine.Businesses.First(b => b.Id == "lemonade").ProgressPercent = 0;
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(16));
        vm.OnTick();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(cashAfterResume);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnResumed_ShouldApplyEarningsBeforeRefreshingUi()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBeforeResume = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(10));
        vm.OnResumed();

        var expectedCash = cashBeforeResume + new BigDouble(1000.0 * LemonadeRevenue(engine));
        engine.Cash.ToDouble().ShouldBe(expectedCash.ToDouble(), tolerance: 1e-9);

        vm.CashText.ShouldBe($"${NumberFormatter.Format(engine.Cash)}");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void OnSuspended_ShouldRecordTimestamp_AndAllowSubsequentResume()
    {
        var (vm, engine, clock, _) = MakeVm();
        var cashBefore = engine.Cash;

        vm.OnSuspended();
        clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30));
        vm.OnResumed();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBeGreaterThan(cashBefore);
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class GameViewModelPrestigeTests
{
    private static (GameViewModel vm, GameEngine engine) MakeVm()
    {
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine();
        return (Ui.CreateGame(engine), engine);
    }

    private static void SetLifetime(GameEngine engine, BigDouble value) => engine.LifetimeEarnings = value;

    [Fact]
    public void PrestigeExplanation_ShowsTheIncomeMultiplierPrestigeWouldGrant()
    {
        var (vm, engine) = MakeVm();
        SetLifetime(engine, new BigDouble(1e16));

        vm.OnTick();

        vm.CanPrestige.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.NextAngelText.ShouldBe("1.50 K");
        vm.PrestigeExplanation.ShouldContain("income \u00D731.00");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PrestigeExplanation_BelowThreshold_AsksToKeepEarning()
    {
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm();

        vm.OnTick();

        vm.CanPrestige.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.PrestigeExplanation.ShouldStartWith("Keep earning");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CrossBonusTexts_AfterLastThreshold_ShowMaxedState()
    {
        var (vm, engine) = MakeVm();
        foreach (var biz in engine.Businesses) biz.Owned = 400;

        vm.OnTick();

        vm.HasCrossSpeedBonus.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.CrossSpeedText.ShouldBe($"\u00D7{NumberFormatter.Format(new BigDouble(CrossBusinessSpeedBonus.MaxMultiplier))} Speed");
        vm.NextCrossThresholdText.ShouldBe("All cross-business bonuses earned");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CrossBonusTexts_BeforeFirstThreshold_ShowRoadmap()
    {
        var (vm, _) = MakeVm();

        vm.OnTick();

        vm.HasCrossSpeedBonus.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.NextCrossThresholdText.ShouldBe("25 more of every business \u2192 25");
    }
}

using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Numerics;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using NSubstitute;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class GameViewModelSettingsTests
{
    private readonly ISoundPlayer _sound = Substitute.For<ISoundPlayer>();
    private readonly FeedbackService _feedback;
    private readonly InMemorySettingsRepository _settings = new(new PlayerSettings(SoundEnabled: false, HapticsEnabled: true));

    public GameViewModelSettingsTests() =>
        _feedback = new FeedbackService(_sound, new NoHaptics(), NullLogger<FeedbackService>.Instance);

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_AppliesSavedSettings()
    {
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(Ui.CreateEngine(), feedback: _feedback, settings: _settings);

        await vm.InitializeAsync();

        vm.SoundEnabled.ShouldBeFalse();
        vm.HapticsEnabled.ShouldBeTrue();
        vm.HapticsSupported.ShouldBeFalse();
        _feedback.SoundEnabled.ShouldBeFalse();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Initialize_IsIdempotent()
    {
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine();
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(engine, feedback: _feedback, settings: _settings);

        await vm.InitializeAsync();
        engine.Cash = new BigDouble(123);
        await vm.InitializeAsync();

        engine.Cash.ShouldBe(new BigDouble(123));
        vm.Businesses.Count.ShouldBe(6);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ToggleSound_PersistsAndPlaysConfirmation()
    {
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(Ui.CreateEngine(), feedback: _feedback, settings: _settings);
        await vm.InitializeAsync();
        var changed = new List<string?>();
        vm.PropertyChanged += (_, e) => changed.Add(e.PropertyName);

        await vm.ToggleSoundCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);

        vm.SoundEnabled.ShouldBeTrue();
        changed.ShouldContain(nameof(vm.SoundEnabled));
        _settings.Current.ShouldBe(new PlayerSettings(SoundEnabled: true, HapticsEnabled: true));
        _sound.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Tap);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Prestige_Unavailable_PlaysDenied()
    {
        var feedback = Substitute.For<IFeedbackService>();
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(Ui.CreateEngine(), feedback: feedback);

        await vm.PrestigeCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);

        feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Denied);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task Prestige_Available_PlaysPrestige()
    {
        var feedback = Substitute.For<IFeedbackService>();
        var engine = Ui.CreateEngine();
        engine.LifetimeEarnings = new BigDouble(1e16);
        var vm = Ui.CreateGame(engine, feedback: feedback);
        vm.OnTick();

        await vm.PrestigeCommand.ExecuteAsync(null);

        feedback.Received(1).Play(FeedbackCue.Prestige);
        engine.PrestigeCount.ShouldBe(1);
    }
}

using Avalonia.Controls.ApplicationLifetimes;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using NSubstitute;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class LifecycleRouterTests
{
    private readonly LifecycleRouter _router = new();
    private readonly ILifecycleAware _target = Substitute.For<ILifecycleAware>();

    [Fact]
    public void Attach_Null_Throws() => Should.Throw<ArgumentNullException>(() => _router.Attach(null!));

    [Fact]
    public void BackgroundDeactivation_SuspendsTargetAndRaisesBackgrounded()
    {
        var backgrounded = 0;
        _router.Backgrounded += (_, _) => backgrounded++;
        _router.Attach(_target);

        _router.OnDeactivated(ActivationKind.Background);

        _target.Received(1).OnSuspended();
        backgrounded.ShouldBe(1);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void BackgroundActivation_ResumesTarget()
    {
        _router.Attach(_target);

        _router.OnActivated(ActivationKind.Background);

        _target.Received(1).OnResumed();
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(ActivationKind.File)]
    [InlineData(ActivationKind.OpenUri)]
    public void NonBackgroundEvents_AreIgnored(ActivationKind kind)
    {
        _router.Attach(_target);

        _router.OnActivated(kind);
        _router.OnDeactivated(kind);

        _target.DidNotReceive().OnResumed();
        _target.DidNotReceive().OnSuspended();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Attach_ReplacesPreviousTarget()
    {
        var replacement = Substitute.For<ILifecycleAware>();
        _router.Attach(_target);
        _router.Attach(replacement);

        _router.OnDeactivated(ActivationKind.Background);

        _target.DidNotReceive().OnSuspended();
        replacement.Received(1).OnSuspended();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Detach_OnlyRemovesMatchingTarget()
    {
        var other = Substitute.For<ILifecycleAware>();
        _router.Attach(_target);

        _router.Detach(other);
        _router.OnActivated(ActivationKind.Background);
        _target.Received(1).OnResumed();

        _router.Detach(_target);
        _router.OnActivated(ActivationKind.Background);
        _target.Received(1).OnResumed();
    }
}

using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class ToastServiceTests
{
    private readonly ManualTimeProvider _clock = new();

    [Fact]
    public void Show_ShouldAddToast()
    {
        var service = new ToastService(_clock);
        service.Show("Test message");
        service.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(1);
        service.ActiveToasts[0].Message.ShouldBe("Test message");
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Show_MultipleTimes_ShouldAddMultiple()
    {
        var service = new ToastService(_clock);
        service.Show("One");
        service.Show("Two");
        service.Show("Three");
        service.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(3);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CleanupExpired_ShouldRemoveExpiredToasts()
    {
        var service = new ToastService(_clock);
        service.Show("Will expire", TimeSpan.Zero);

        service.CleanupExpired();

        service.ActiveToasts.ShouldBeEmpty();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CleanupExpired_DefaultLifetime_ExpiresAfterThreeSeconds()
    {
        var service = new ToastService(_clock);
        service.Show("Default");

        _clock.Advance(ToastService.DefaultLifetime - TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1));
        service.CleanupExpired();
        service.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(1);

        _clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(1));
        service.CleanupExpired();
        service.ActiveToasts.ShouldBeEmpty();
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CleanupExpired_MixedToasts_ShouldOnlyRemoveExpired()
    {
        var service = new ToastService(_clock);
        service.Show("Expired", TimeSpan.Zero);
        service.Show("Active", TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5));

        _clock.Advance(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
        service.CleanupExpired();

        service.ActiveToasts.Count.ShouldBe(1);
        service.ActiveToasts[0].Message.ShouldBe("Active");
    }
}

FILE: tests/MyAdventure.UI.Tests/ToneSynthesizerTests.cs
SIZE: 2.49 KB

using System.Buffers.Binary;
using System.Text;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using Shouldly;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

public class ToneSynthesizerTests
{
    [Theory]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Tap)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Purchase)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Manager)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Milestone)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Prestige)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Denied)]
    public void Render_ProducesValidMono16BitPcmWave(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        var wav = ToneSynthesizer.Render(cue);
        var span = wav.AsSpan();

        Encoding.ASCII.GetString(wav, 0, 4).ShouldBe("RIFF");
        Encoding.ASCII.GetString(wav, 8, 4).ShouldBe("WAVE");
        Encoding.ASCII.GetString(wav, 36, 4).ShouldBe("data");
        BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32LittleEndian(span[4..]).ShouldBe(wav.Length - 8);
        BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt16LittleEndian(span[22..]).ShouldBe((short)1);
        BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32LittleEndian(span[24..]).ShouldBe(ToneSynthesizer.SampleRate);
        BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt16LittleEndian(span[34..]).ShouldBe((short)16);
        BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32LittleEndian(span[40..]).ShouldBe(wav.Length - ToneSynthesizer.HeaderSize);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Tap)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Purchase)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Manager)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Milestone)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Prestige)]
    [InlineData(FeedbackCue.Denied)]
    public void Render_IsShortAudibleAndUnclipped(FeedbackCue cue)
    {
        var data = ToneSynthesizer.Render(cue).AsSpan(ToneSynthesizer.HeaderSize);
        var samples = data.Length / sizeof(short);
        var seconds = (double)samples / ToneSynthesizer.SampleRate;

        seconds.ShouldBeInRange(0.01, 0.6);

        var peak = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < samples; i++)
        {
            peak = Math.Max(peak, Math.Abs((int)BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt16LittleEndian(data[(i * 2)..])));
        }

        peak.ShouldBeGreaterThan(1000);
        peak.ShouldBeLessThan(short.MaxValue / 2);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void Render_EveryCueIsCovered() =>
        Enum.GetValues<FeedbackCue>().Length.ShouldBe(6);

    [Fact]
    public void Render_IsDeterministic() =>
        ToneSynthesizer.Render(FeedbackCue.Milestone).SequenceEqual(ToneSynthesizer.Render(FeedbackCue.Milestone)).ShouldBeTrue();

    [Fact]
    public void Render_CuesSoundDifferent() =>
        ToneSynthesizer.Render(FeedbackCue.Purchase).SequenceEqual(ToneSynthesizer.Render(FeedbackCue.Denied)).ShouldBeFalse();
}

FILE: tests/MyAdventure.UI.Tests/Ui.cs

using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using MyAdventure.Core.Entities;
using MyAdventure.Core.Interfaces;
using MyAdventure.Core.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Feedback;
using MyAdventure.Shared.Services;
using MyAdventure.Shared.ViewModels;
using NSubstitute;

namespace MyAdventure.UI.Tests;

internal static class Ui
{
    public static GameEngine CreateEngine(TimeProvider? time = null, IGameStateRepository? repository = null) =>
        new(repository ?? new InMemoryGameStateRepository(), NullLogger<GameEngine>.Instance, time);

    public static GameViewModel CreateGame(
        GameEngine engine,
        TimeProvider? time = null,
        ToastService? toasts = null,
        IFeedbackService? feedback = null,
        ISettingsRepository? settings = null)
    {
        var clock = time ?? TimeProvider.System;
        return new GameViewModel(
            engine,
            toasts ?? new ToastService(clock),
            settings ?? new InMemorySettingsRepository(),
            feedback ?? Substitute.For<IFeedbackService>(),
            NullLogger<GameViewModel>.Instance,
            clock);
    }

    public static BusinessViewModel CreateBusiness(
        Business business,
        GameEngine engine,
        IFeedbackService? feedback = null,
        ToastService? toasts = null) =>
        new(business, engine, toasts ?? new ToastService(), feedback ?? Substitute.For<IFeedbackService>());
}
